===== game-track/godot/scripts/sealed_lighthouse_machine.gd | sha256:48da35d485473abfa9805e0dddb80d6aa6bad5c0bd183c57541886e768f839c1 | bytes:7231 =====
class_name SealedLighthouseMachine
extends RefCounted

const CanonicalState = preload("res://scripts/canonical_state.gd")

var scenario: Dictionary
var state: Dictionary


func _init(scenario_document: Dictionary) -> void:
	scenario = scenario_document.duplicate(true)
	state = _normalize_state(scenario["initial_state"])


func reset() -> void:
	state = _normalize_state(scenario["initial_state"])


func load_state(next_state: Dictionary) -> void:
	state = _normalize_state(next_state)


func load_state_if_hash_matches(next_state: Dictionary, expected_hash: String) -> bool:
	# Validate the candidate snapshot before replacing the live canonical state.
	# A corrupt save must not become authoritative even briefly.
	var normalized := _normalize_state(next_state)
	if CanonicalState.sha256(normalized) != expected_hash:
		return false
	state = normalized
	return true


func state_hash() -> String:
	return CanonicalState.sha256(state)


func _normalize_state(document: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	# Godot's JSON reader may represent integral JSON numbers as floats. Normalize the
	# schema-declared integer fields before hashing, save/load comparison, and replay.
	var normalized := document.duplicate(true)
	normalized["revision"] = int(normalized["revision"])
	normalized["quest"]["stage"] = int(normalized["quest"]["stage"])
	return normalized


func validate_disclosure(disclosed_facts: Array) -> Array:
	var codes: Array = []
	var policy: Dictionary = scenario["disclosure_policy"]
	for fact in disclosed_facts:
		if fact in policy["permanently_forbidden"]:
			codes.append("FORBIDDEN_DISCLOSURE")
		for gate in policy["stage_gates"]:
			if gate["fact_id"] == fact and state["quest"]["stage"] < gate["minimum_stage"]:
				codes.append("STAGE_GATED_DISCLOSURE")
	return CanonicalState.sorted_unique(codes)


func apply_operation(operation: String, arguments: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var prior := state.duplicate(true)
	var next := state.duplicate(true)
	var codes: Array = []

	match operation:
		"acquire_object":
			codes = _acquire_object(next, arguments)
		"install_lens":
			codes = _install_lens(next, arguments)
		"reveal_hint":
			codes = _reveal_hint(next, arguments)
		_:
			codes = ["UNKNOWN_OPERATION"]

	if not codes.is_empty():
		return {
			"accepted": false,
			"codes": CanonicalState.sorted_unique(codes),
			"prior_state": prior,
			"state": prior.duplicate(true),
			"mutated": false,
		}

	next["revision"] = int(prior["revision"]) + 1
	state = next
	return {
		"accepted": true,
		"codes": [],
		"prior_state": prior,
		"state": next.duplicate(true),
		"mutated": CanonicalState.sha256(prior) != CanonicalState.sha256(next),
	}
func apply_world_event(event_code: String, arguments: Dictionary = {}) -> Dictionary:
	var prior := state.duplicate(true)
	var next := state.duplicate(true)
	var codes: Array = []
	var preconditions := {
		"event_code": event_code,
		"arguments_empty": arguments.is_empty(),
		"lens_present": next["world"]["object_locations"].has("signal_lens"),
		"lens_not_carried": "signal_lens" not in next["player"]["inventory"],
		"lamp_store_reachable": "lamp_store" in next["world"]["reachable_locations"],
		"quest_stage": int(next["quest"]["stage"]),
	}
	match event_code:
		"LENS_UNREACHABLE":
			if not arguments.is_empty():
				codes.append("WORLD_EVENT_ARGUMENTS_INVALID")
			if not bool(preconditions["lens_present"]):
				codes.append("WORLD_EVENT_LENS_MISSING")
			if not bool(preconditions["lens_not_carried"]):
				codes.append("WORLD_EVENT_LENS_ALREADY_CARRIED")
			if not bool(preconditions["lamp_store_reachable"]):
				codes.append("WORLD_EVENT_ALREADY_APPLIED")
			if int(preconditions["quest_stage"]) != 0:
				codes.append("WORLD_EVENT_STAGE_PRECONDITION")
			if codes.is_empty():
				next["world"]["reachable_locations"].erase("lamp_store")
				next["world"]["reachable_locations"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["world"]["reachable_locations"])
		_:
			codes.append("UNSUPPORTED_WORLD_EVENT")
	if not codes.is_empty():
		return {
			"accepted": false,
			"codes": CanonicalState.sorted_unique(codes),
			"event_code": event_code,
			"preconditions": preconditions,
			"prior_state": prior,
			"state": prior.duplicate(true),
			"mutated": false,
		}
	next["revision"] = int(prior["revision"]) + 1
	state = next
	return {
		"accepted": true,
		"codes": [],
		"event_code": event_code,
		"preconditions": preconditions,
		"prior_state": prior,
		"state": next.duplicate(true),
		"mutated": CanonicalState.sha256(prior) != CanonicalState.sha256(next),
		"trace": {
			"event_code": event_code,
			"revision_before": int(prior["revision"]),
			"revision_after": int(next["revision"]),
			"preconditions": preconditions,
		},
	}


func _acquire_object(next: Dictionary, arguments: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var object_id: String = arguments.get("object_id", "")
	var object_locations: Dictionary = next["world"]["object_locations"]
	if not object_locations.has(object_id):
		return ["OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT"]
	var location: String = object_locations[object_id]
	if location not in next["world"]["reachable_locations"]:
		return ["OBJECT_NOT_REACHABLE"]
	object_locations.erase(object_id)
	next["player"]["inventory"].append(object_id)
	next["player"]["inventory"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["player"]["inventory"])
	next["facts"].append("signal_lens_acquired")
	next["facts"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["facts"])
	next["quest"]["stage"] = maxi(int(next["quest"]["stage"]), 1)
	next["quest"]["flags"].append("signal_lens_acquired")
	next["quest"]["flags"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["quest"]["flags"])
	return []


func _install_lens(next: Dictionary, arguments: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var object_id: String = arguments.get("object_id", "")
	if object_id not in next["player"]["inventory"]:
		return ["MISSING_REQUIRED_OBJECT"]
	if int(next["quest"]["stage"]) < 1:
		return ["QUEST_STAGE_PRECONDITION"]
	next["facts"].append("signal_lens_installed")
	next["facts"].append("lighthouse_hint_authorized")
	next["facts"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["facts"])
	next["quest"]["stage"] = 2
	next["quest"]["flags"].append("signal_lens_installed")
	next["quest"]["flags"].append("lighthouse_hint_authorized")
	next["quest"]["flags"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["quest"]["flags"])
	return []


func _reveal_hint(next: Dictionary, arguments: Dictionary) -> Array:
	var actor_id: String = arguments.get("actor_id", "")
	var fact_id: String = arguments.get("fact_id", "")
	var disclosure_codes := validate_disclosure([fact_id])
	if not disclosure_codes.is_empty():
		return disclosure_codes
	if actor_id != "captain_mira":
		return ["UNKNOWN_ACTOR"]
	if fact_id not in next["npcs"][actor_id]["knowledge"]:
		return ["NPC_DOES_NOT_KNOW_FACT"]
	next["facts"].append(fact_id)
	next["facts"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(next["facts"])
	next["npcs"][actor_id]["disclosed"].append(fact_id)
	next["npcs"][actor_id]["disclosed"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(
		next["npcs"][actor_id]["disclosed"]
	)
	next["npcs"][actor_id]["relationship_memory"].append(
		"turn-05:mira-shared-tide-marks-hint"
	)
	next["npcs"][actor_id]["relationship_memory"] = CanonicalState.sorted_unique(
		next["npcs"][actor_id]["relationship_memory"]
	)
	return []

===== END game-track/godot/scripts/sealed_lighthouse_machine.gd =====

===== scripts/run_npc_service.py | sha256:421b475a2fd1591f74fe70f982d86cda9f0846dbcd0b71dad4312cb254907441 | bytes:31069 =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run the fixed-goal NPC interpreter/planner over a bounded loopback HTTP service.

Examples:
  python scripts/run_npc_service.py --backend codex --model gpt-5
  python scripts/run_npc_service.py --backend rule --trace-output /tmp/npc-trace.jsonl

The Codex backend is explicit and fail-closed.  Rule/reference arms are research controls,
never hidden fallbacks.  Godot owns all canonical world writes; /npc/result only acknowledges
an engine outcome and invalidates stale Python plans.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import signal
import socket
import sys
import threading
from collections.abc import Mapping
from http import HTTPStatus
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SRC_ROOT = PROJECT_ROOT / "src"
if str(SRC_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(SRC_ROOT))

from nesy_game.npc_goal import (
    SCHEMA_VERSION,
    BudgetExceeded,
    ControllerFailure,
    GoalController,
    IntentInterpreter,
    ProtocolError,
    ReplayValidator,
    content_hash,
    validate_envelope,
)

MAX_JSON_DEPTH = 32
MAX_INFERENCE_CALLS = 8
HEADER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 5.0
BODY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 5.0
CAPABILITY_HEADER = "X-NPC-Capability"
CANONICAL_CONFIG_PATH = PROJECT_ROOT / "configs" / "npc-goal-recovery.json"


def _validate_loopback_origin(origin: Any) -> str:
    if not isinstance(origin, str):
        raise ProtocolError("canonical_origin_must_be_string")
    parsed = urlsplit(origin)
    try:
        port = parsed.port
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError("canonical_origin_invalid_port") from exc
    if (
        parsed.scheme != "http"
        or parsed.hostname not in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost"}
        or port is None
        or not 1 <= port <= 65535
        or origin != f"http://{parsed.hostname}:{port}"
    ):
        raise ValueError("canonical_origin_must_be_exact_loopback_http")
    return origin


def _load_canonical_transport() -> dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        config_bytes = CANONICAL_CONFIG_PATH.read_bytes()
        config = json.loads(config_bytes.decode("utf-8"))
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_unavailable") from exc
    if not isinstance(config, dict) or config.get("schema_version") != SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_schema_mismatch")
    transport = config.get("transport")
    if not isinstance(transport, dict):
        raise ProtocolError("canonical_transport_config_invalid")
    bind = transport.get("bind")
    port = transport.get("port")
    if (
        bind not in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost"}
        or isinstance(port, bool)
        or not isinstance(port, int)
        or not 1 <= port <= 65535
    ):
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_invalid")
    expected_paths = {
        "step_path": "/npc/step",
        "result_path": "/npc/result",
        "health_path": "/health",
    }
    if any(transport.get(key) != value for key, value in expected_paths.items()):
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_route_mismatch")
    body_limit = transport.get("body_limit_bytes")
    if isinstance(body_limit, bool) or not isinstance(body_limit, int) or body_limit < 1:
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_body_limit_invalid")
    raw_origins = transport.get("default_allowed_origins")
    if not isinstance(raw_origins, list) or not raw_origins:
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_origins_invalid")
    origins = frozenset(_validate_loopback_origin(origin) for origin in raw_origins)
    if len(origins) != len(raw_origins):
        raise ValueError("canonical_transport_config_origins_duplicate")
    return {
        "bind": bind,
        "port": port,
        "body_limit": body_limit,
        "origins": origins,
        "config_hash": hashlib.sha256(config_bytes).hexdigest(),
    }


_CANONICAL_TRANSPORT = _load_canonical_transport()
BODY_LIMIT = _CANONICAL_TRANSPORT["body_limit"]
DEFAULT_SERVICE_HOST = _CANONICAL_TRANSPORT["bind"]
DEFAULT_SERVICE_PORT = _CANONICAL_TRANSPORT["port"]
DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS = _CANONICAL_TRANSPORT["origins"]
CANONICAL_CONFIG_HASH = _CANONICAL_TRANSPORT["config_hash"]
_INTEGER_FIELDS = ("session_epoch", "sequence", "delivery_index")
_BUDGET_INTEGER_FIELDS = (
    "intent_deadline_ms",
    "episode_deadline_ms",
    "max_intent_calls",
    "max_action_attempts",
    "max_replans",
    "max_plan_expansions",
    "max_inflight_per_session",
)


def _json_depth_within_limit(value: Any, limit: int = MAX_JSON_DEPTH) -> bool:
    pending: list[tuple[Any, int]] = [(value, 1)]
    while pending:
        current, depth = pending.pop()
        if depth > limit:
            return False
        if isinstance(current, dict):
            pending.extend((item, depth + 1) for item in current.values())
        elif isinstance(current, list):
            pending.extend((item, depth + 1) for item in current)
    return True


def _prepare_payload(payload: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise ProtocolError("payload_must_be_object")
    if "message_type" in payload and not isinstance(payload["message_type"], str):
        raise ProtocolError("invalid_payload")
    for key in _INTEGER_FIELDS:
        if isinstance(payload.get(key), bool):
            raise ProtocolError(f"invalid_{key}")
    state_ref = payload.get("state_ref")
    if isinstance(state_ref, dict) and isinstance(state_ref.get("revision"), bool):
        raise ProtocolError("invalid_state_revision")
    budget = payload.get("budget")
    if isinstance(budget, dict):
        for key in _BUDGET_INTEGER_FIELDS:
            if isinstance(budget.get(key), bool):
                raise ProtocolError(f"invalid_budget_{key}")
        if any(budget.get(key) is not None for key in ("input_tokens", "output_tokens", "cost")):
            safe = dict(payload)
            safe_budget = dict(budget)
            for key in ("input_tokens", "output_tokens", "cost"):
                safe_budget[key] = None
            safe["budget"] = safe_budget
            safe["content_sha256"] = content_hash(safe)
            return safe
    return payload


def _reject_json_constant(value: str) -> None:
    raise ValueError(f"invalid_json_constant:{value}")


class ServiceState:
    def __init__(
        self,
        controller: GoalController,
        *,
        allowed_origins: frozenset[str],
        max_workers: int,
        trace_output: Path | None,
        capability: str,
        allowed_hosts: frozenset[str] | None = None,
        max_inference_calls: int = MAX_INFERENCE_CALLS,
        header_timeout: float = HEADER_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
        body_timeout: float = BODY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
    ) -> None:
        if max_workers < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_workers must be positive")
        if max_inference_calls < 1:
            raise ValueError("max_inference_calls must be positive")
        if not capability:
            raise ValueError("capability_required")
        if len(capability) > 256:
            raise ValueError("capability is too long")
        if header_timeout <= 0 or body_timeout <= 0:
            raise ValueError("timeouts must be positive")
        self.controller = controller
        self.allowed_origins = allowed_origins
        self.allowed_hosts = allowed_hosts or frozenset()
        self.capability = capability
        self.max_workers = max_workers
        self.header_timeout = float(header_timeout)
        self.body_timeout = float(body_timeout)
        self.trace_output = trace_output
        self.trace_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.trace_capacity_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.trace_exported_cursors: dict[tuple[str, int], dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self.trace_rows_total = 0
        self.trace_rows_reserved = 0
        self._trace_reservation_seq = 0
        self._trace_reservations: dict[int, tuple[tuple[str, int], int]] = {}
        self._trace_reservations_by_key: dict[tuple[str, int], list[int]] = {}
        self._trace_local = threading.local()
        self.inference_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.inference_calls = 0
        self.max_inference_calls = max_inference_calls
        self.shutdown = threading.Event()
        self.controller.before_inference = self.reserve_inference
        self.controller.before_session = self.reserve_trace
        self.controller.before_decision = self.reserve_trace
        self.controller.before_result = self.reserve_trace

    def set_allowed_hosts(self, hosts: frozenset[str]) -> None:
        self.allowed_hosts = hosts

    def reserve_inference(self) -> None:
        with self.inference_lock:
            if self.inference_calls >= self.max_inference_calls:
                raise BudgetExceeded("aggregate_inference_quota_exceeded")
            self.inference_calls += 1

    def reserve_trace(self, session_id: str, session_epoch: int, rows: int) -> None:
        if self.trace_output is None:
            return
        if rows < 1:
            raise ValueError("trace reservation must be positive")
        key = (session_id, session_epoch)
        with self.trace_capacity_lock:
            if self.trace_rows_total + self.trace_rows_reserved + rows > 4096:
                raise ControllerFailure("trace_overflow")
            self._trace_reservation_seq += 1
            token = self._trace_reservation_seq
            self._trace_reservations[token] = (key, rows)
            self._trace_reservations_by_key.setdefault(key, []).append(token)
            self.trace_rows_reserved += rows
            tokens = list(getattr(self._trace_local, "tokens", []))
            tokens.append(token)
            self._trace_local.tokens = tokens

    def _release_trace_reservation_locked(self, token: int) -> None:
        reservation = self._trace_reservations.pop(token, None)
        if reservation is None:
            return
        key, rows = reservation
        tokens = self._trace_reservations_by_key.get(key)
        if tokens is not None:
            try:
                tokens.remove(token)
            except ValueError:
                pass
            if not tokens:
                self._trace_reservations_by_key.pop(key, None)
        self.trace_rows_reserved -= rows
        local_tokens = [
            value for value in getattr(self._trace_local, "tokens", []) if value != token
        ]
        self._trace_local.tokens = local_tokens

    def release_trace_reservation(self, session_id: str, session_epoch: int) -> None:
        key = (session_id, session_epoch)
        with self.trace_capacity_lock:
            tokens = list(getattr(self._trace_local, "tokens", []))
            token = next(
                (
                    value
                    for value in reversed(tokens)
                    if self._trace_reservations.get(value, (None, 0))[0] == key
                ),
                None,
            )
            if token is not None:
                self._release_trace_reservation_locked(token)

    def _trace_reservation_for_key_locked(self, key: tuple[str, int]) -> int | None:
        tokens = list(getattr(self._trace_local, "tokens", []))
        for token in reversed(tokens):
            if self._trace_reservations.get(token, (None, 0))[0] == key:
                return token
        return None

    def write_trace(self, session_id: str, session_epoch: int) -> None:
        if self.trace_output is None:
            return
        with self.trace_lock:
            snapshot = self.controller.export_trace(session_id, session_epoch=session_epoch)
            if not isinstance(snapshot, Mapping):
                raise ControllerFailure("trace_snapshot_invalid")
            snapshot_id = snapshot.get("snapshot_id")
            snapshot_session_id = snapshot.get("session_id")
            snapshot_epoch = snapshot.get("session_epoch")
            cursor = snapshot.get("cursor")
            rows = snapshot.get("rows")
            if (
                not isinstance(snapshot_id, str)
                or not snapshot_id
                or snapshot_session_id != session_id
                or snapshot_epoch != session_epoch
                or not isinstance(cursor, Mapping)
                or not isinstance(rows, list)
                or any(not isinstance(row, Mapping) for row in rows)
                or cursor.get("session_id") != session_id
                or cursor.get("session_epoch") != session_epoch
                or isinstance(cursor.get("row_count"), bool)
                or not isinstance(cursor.get("row_count"), int)
                or cursor["row_count"] < 0
                or cursor["row_count"] != len(rows)
                or not isinstance(cursor.get("head_hash"), str)
            ):
                raise ControllerFailure("trace_snapshot_invalid")
            if rows and rows[-1].get("hash") != cursor["head_hash"]:
                raise ControllerFailure("trace_cursor_mismatch")
            if len(rows) > 4096:
                raise ControllerFailure("trace_overflow")

            key = (session_id, session_epoch)
            prior = self.trace_exported_cursors.get(key)
            start = 0
            if prior is not None:
                prior_count = prior["row_count"]
                if prior_count > len(rows):
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_cursor_mismatch")
                if prior_count and rows[prior_count - 1].get("hash") != prior["head_hash"]:
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_cursor_mismatch")
                if prior["snapshot_id"] == snapshot_id and (
                    cursor["row_count"] != prior_count or cursor["head_hash"] != prior["head_hash"]
                ):
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_cursor_mismatch")
                if prior_count == len(rows) and prior["snapshot_id"] != snapshot_id:
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_cursor_mismatch")
                start = prior_count
            new_rows = rows[start:]
            if new_rows:
                with self.trace_capacity_lock:
                    has_reservation = self._trace_reservation_for_key_locked(key) is not None
                if not has_reservation:
                    self.reserve_trace(session_id, session_epoch, len(new_rows))
            with self.trace_capacity_lock:
                token = self._trace_reservation_for_key_locked(key)
                reserved = (
                    self._trace_reservations.get(token, (key, 0))[1] if token is not None else 0
                )
                if token is not None and len(new_rows) > reserved:
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_overflow")
                if self.trace_rows_total + len(new_rows) > 4096:
                    raise ControllerFailure("trace_overflow")
                if not new_rows:
                    if token is not None:
                        self._release_trace_reservation_locked(token)
                    return
            self.trace_output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with self.trace_output.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                for row in new_rows:
                    record = {
                        "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
                        "session_id": session_id,
                        "session_epoch": session_epoch,
                        "snapshot_id": snapshot_id,
                        "row": row,
                    }
                    handle.write(
                        json.dumps(
                            record, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
                        )
                        + "\n"
                    )
            self.trace_exported_cursors[key] = {
                "session_id": session_id,
                "session_epoch": session_epoch,
                "snapshot_id": snapshot_id,
                "row_count": cursor["row_count"],
                "head_hash": cursor["head_hash"],
            }
            with self.trace_capacity_lock:
                self.trace_rows_total += len(new_rows)
                if token is not None:
                    self._release_trace_reservation_locked(token)


class NpcHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version = "npc-goal-service/1.1"
    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"

    @property
    def state(self) -> ServiceState:
        return self.server.service_state  # type: ignore[attr-defined]

    def setup(self) -> None:
        super().setup()
        self.connection.settimeout(self.state.header_timeout)

    def handle(self) -> None:
        try:
            super().handle()
        except TimeoutError:
            self.close_connection = True

    def log_message(self, _format: str, *_args: Any) -> None:
        return

    def _origin_allowed(self) -> bool:
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        return origin is None or origin in self.state.allowed_origins

    def _host_allowed(self) -> bool:
        hosts = self.headers.get_all("Host", [])
        return len(hosts) == 1 and hosts[0] in self.state.allowed_hosts

    def _capability_allowed(self) -> bool:
        supplied = self.headers.get(CAPABILITY_HEADER)
        expected = f"Bearer {self.state.capability}"
        return supplied is not None and hmac.compare_digest(supplied, expected)

    def _send_json(
        self, status: int, payload: Mapping[str, Any], *, origin: str | None = None
    ) -> None:
        self.close_connection = True
        encoded = json.dumps(
            payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
        ).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(encoded)))
        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
        self.send_header("Connection", "close")
        if origin in self.state.allowed_origins:
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
            self.send_header("Vary", "Origin")
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, GET, OPTIONS")
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, X-NPC-Capability")
        self.end_headers()
        try:
            self.wfile.write(encoded)
        except OSError:
            self.close_connection = True

    def _error(self, status: int, code: str) -> None:
        self._send_json(
            status,
            {"error": code, "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION},
            origin=self.headers.get("Origin"),
        )

    def _check_route_access(self, *, capability_required: bool) -> bool:
        if not self._host_allowed():
            self._error(HTTPStatus.FORBIDDEN, "host_not_allowed")
            return False
        if not self._origin_allowed():
            self._error(HTTPStatus.FORBIDDEN, "origin_not_allowed")
            return False
        if capability_required and not self._capability_allowed():
            self._error(HTTPStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "capability_required")
            return False
        return True

    def do_OPTIONS(self) -> None:
        if not self._check_route_access(capability_required=False):
            return
        self.close_connection = True
        self.send_response(HTTPStatus.NO_CONTENT)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", "0")
        self.send_header("Connection", "close")
        origin = self.headers.get("Origin")
        if origin in self.state.allowed_origins:
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin)
            self.send_header("Vary", "Origin")
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "POST, GET, OPTIONS")
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, X-NPC-Capability")
        self.end_headers()

    def do_GET(self) -> None:
        if not self._check_route_access(capability_required=False):
            return
        path = urlsplit(self.path).path
        if path != "/health":
            self._error(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, "not_found")
            return
        self._send_json(
            HTTPStatus.OK,
            {
                "status": "ok",
                "schema_version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
                "config_hash": CANONICAL_CONFIG_HASH,
                "service": "npc-goal",
            },
            origin=self.headers.get("Origin"),
        )

    def do_POST(self) -> None:
        path = urlsplit(self.path).path
        if path not in {"/npc/step", "/npc/result", "/npc/replay"}:
            self._check_route_access(capability_required=False)
            if not self.close_connection:
                self._error(HTTPStatus.NOT_FOUND, "not_found")
            return
        if not self._check_route_access(capability_required=path != "/npc/replay"):
            return
        if self.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding"):
            self._error(HTTPStatus.LENGTH_REQUIRED, "transfer_encoding_not_allowed")
            return
        if self.headers.get("Content-Type") != "application/json":
            self._error(HTTPStatus.UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE, "content_type_must_be_application_json")
            return
        try:
            content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "-1"))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            content_length = -1
        if content_length < 0 or content_length > BODY_LIMIT:
            self._error(HTTPStatus.REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE, "body_limit_exceeded")
            return
        payload: Any = None
        envelope: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
        try:
            self.connection.settimeout(self.state.body_timeout)
            raw = self.rfile.read(content_length)
            if len(raw) != content_length:
                self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "incomplete_body")
                return
            try:
                payload = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"), parse_constant=_reject_json_constant)
            except (UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError, RecursionError):
                self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "invalid_json")
                return
            if not _json_depth_within_limit(payload):
                self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "json_nesting_too_deep")
                return
            if path == "/npc/replay":
                self._handle_replay(payload)
                return
            expected = "request" if path == "/npc/step" else "result"
            envelope = validate_envelope(_prepare_payload(payload), expected_message_type=expected)
            if envelope["config_hash"] != CANONICAL_CONFIG_HASH:
                raise ProtocolError("config_hash_mismatch")
            if expected == "request":
                response = self.state.controller.handle_request(envelope)
            else:
                response = self.state.controller.handle_result(envelope)
            try:
                self.state.write_trace(envelope["session_id"], envelope["session_epoch"])
            except (ControllerFailure, OSError) as exc:
                self._error(HTTPStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, f"trace_write_failed:{exc}")
                return
            self._send_json(HTTPStatus.OK, response, origin=self.headers.get("Origin"))
        except ProtocolError as exc:
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, str(exc))
        except BudgetExceeded as exc:
            self._send_controller_error(payload, str(exc), HTTPStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS)
        except ControllerFailure as exc:
            self._send_controller_error(payload, str(exc), HTTPStatus.CONFLICT)
        except TypeError:
            self._send_controller_error(payload, "invalid_payload", HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError, KeyError, RecursionError):
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "invalid_payload")
        except TimeoutError:
            self.close_connection = True
        finally:
            if envelope is not None:
                self.state.release_trace_reservation(
                    envelope["session_id"], envelope["session_epoch"]
                )

    def _handle_replay(self, payload: Any) -> None:
        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or set(payload) != {"records", "expected_session_id"}:
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "replay_payload_keys")
            return
        records = payload["records"]
        expected = payload["expected_session_id"]
        if (
            not isinstance(records, list)
            or any(not isinstance(record, dict) for record in records)
            or (expected is not None and not isinstance(expected, str))
        ):
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "invalid_replay_payload")
            return
        try:
            result = ReplayValidator.validate(records, expected_session_id=expected)
        except ProtocolError as exc:
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, str(exc))
            return
        except (ControllerFailure, TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError, KeyError):
            self._error(HTTPStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "invalid_replay_payload")
            return
        self._send_json(HTTPStatus.OK, result, origin=self.headers.get("Origin"))

    def _send_controller_error(self, payload: Any, code: str, status: int) -> None:
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            try:
                valid = validate_envelope(_prepare_payload(payload))
                ack = GoalController.make_envelope(
                    valid,
                    "ack",
                    {"ack_for": valid["message_type"], "status": "REJECTED", "error": code},
                )
                self._send_json(status, ack, origin=self.headers.get("Origin"))
                return
            except (ProtocolError, TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError, KeyError):
                pass
        self._error(status, code)


class NpcServer(ThreadingHTTPServer):
    allow_reuse_address = True
    daemon_threads = True

    def __init__(self, host: str, port: int, state: ServiceState) -> None:
        super().__init__((host, port), NpcHandler)
        self.service_state = state
        self.connection_slots = threading.BoundedSemaphore(state.max_workers)
        if not state.allowed_hosts:
            state.set_allowed_hosts(frozenset({f"{host}:{self.server_port}"}))

    def process_request(self, request: socket.socket, client_address: Any) -> None:
        if not self.connection_slots.acquire(blocking=False):
            self.close_request(request)
            return
        try:
            thread = threading.Thread(
                target=self.process_request_thread,
                args=(request, client_address),
                daemon=self.daemon_threads,
            )
            thread.start()
        except BaseException:
            self.connection_slots.release()
            self.close_request(request)
            raise

    def process_request_thread(self, request: socket.socket, client_address: Any) -> None:
        try:
            super().process_request_thread(request, client_address)
        finally:
            self.connection_slots.release()


def validate_replay(
    records: list[Mapping[str, Any]], *, expected_session_id: str | None = None
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Evaluator callable: validate recorded envelopes without rerunning Codex."""
    return ReplayValidator.validate(records, expected_session_id=expected_session_id)


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    canonical = _load_canonical_transport()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--host",
        default=canonical["bind"],
        help=f"loopback bind host (default: {canonical['bind']})",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--port",
        type=int,
        default=canonical["port"],
        help=f"loopback bind port (default: {canonical['port']})",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--backend",
        choices=("codex", "rule", "reference"),
        default="codex",
        help="explicit interpreter arm; no hidden fallback",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--model", default=None, help="Codex model identifier; unavailable accounting remains null"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--trace-output",
        type=Path,
        default=None,
        help="opt-in JSONL trace destination; raw utterances are never written",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cache-ttl", type=float, default=30.0, help="bounded response cache TTL seconds (0-3600)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-workers", type=int, default=4, help="bounded concurrent request workers (1-32)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--allow-origin",
        action="append",
        dest="origins",
        help="additional exact loopback origin; repeatable",
    )
    return parser


def run_service(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    canonical = _load_canonical_transport()
    global BODY_LIMIT, CANONICAL_CONFIG_HASH
    BODY_LIMIT = canonical["body_limit"]
    CANONICAL_CONFIG_HASH = canonical["config_hash"]
    extra_origins = frozenset(_validate_loopback_origin(origin) for origin in (args.origins or ()))
    origins = canonical["origins"] | extra_origins
    if args.host != canonical["bind"]:
        raise ValueError("service bind must match canonical transport config")
    if not 1 <= args.port <= 65535 or not 1 <= args.max_workers <= 32:
        raise ValueError("port/max-workers out of range")
    if args.cache_ttl < 0 or args.cache_ttl > 3600:
        raise ValueError("cache-ttl out of range")
    capability = os.environ.get("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY")
    if not capability:
        raise ValueError("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY is required")
    arm = {"codex": "P", "rule": "R", "reference": "O"}[args.backend]
    interpreter = IntentInterpreter(arm=arm, model=args.model)
    controller = GoalController(interpreter=interpreter, cache_ttl_seconds=args.cache_ttl)
    state = ServiceState(
        controller,
        allowed_origins=origins,
        max_workers=args.max_workers,
        trace_output=args.trace_output,
        capability=capability,
    )
    server = NpcServer(args.host, args.port, state)

    def stop(_signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
        state.shutdown.set()
        threading.Thread(target=server.shutdown, daemon=True).start()

    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, stop)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, stop)
    try:
        server.serve_forever(poll_interval=0.25)
    finally:
        server.server_close()
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        return run_service(args)
    except ValueError as exc:
        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

===== END scripts/run_npc_service.py =====

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Use the authenticated Codex CLI as a local, soft-proposal-only LLM companion.

Authentication and credential storage remain wholly owned by the official Codex
CLI.  This wrapper never opens, copies, or logs credential files or tokens.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
OUTPUT_SCHEMA = PROJECT_ROOT / "game-track" / "schemas" / "codex-oauth-soft-proposal.schema.json"
CODEX = "codex"
REQUEST_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$")
PROPOSAL_KEYS = {
    "schema_version",
    "request_id",
    "status",
    "claim_boundary",
    "authorization_effect",
    "canonical_state_mutated",
    "hard_validation_required",
    "response",
    "assumptions",
    "uncertainties",
}
SMOKE_PROMPT = (
    "Reply briefly in Korean and English that this is a non-authoritative soft "
    "proposal and cannot authorize a game action."
)

Runner = Callable[..., subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]]

MAX_SUBPROCESS_OUTPUT = 64 * 1024
MAX_FINAL_MESSAGE_BYTES = 64 * 1024
TOOL_POLICY_FEATURES = (
    "shell_tool",
    "shell_snapshot",
    "unified_exec",
    "browser_use",
    "browser_use_external",
    "browser_use_full_cdp_access",
    "computer_use",
    "apps",
    "enable_mcp_apps",
    "mcp_2026_07_28",
    "tool_call_mcp_elicitation",
    "tool_suggest",
    "skill_search",
    "skill_mcp_dependency_install",
    "image_generation",
    "view_image",
    "in_app_browser",
    "code_mode_host",
)


def _output_within_limit(completed: subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]) -> bool:
    return all(
        len(text or "") <= MAX_SUBPROCESS_OUTPUT for text in (completed.stdout, completed.stderr)
    )


def _tool_policy_flags(runner: Runner | None) -> list[str] | None:
    """Return explicit feature disables, or None when this CLI cannot enforce them."""
    if runner is not None:
        return []
    executable = _codex_executable(None)
    try:
        help_result = subprocess.run(
            [executable, "exec", "--help"],
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=10,
        )
        feature_result = subprocess.run(
            [executable, "features", "list"],
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=10,
        )
    except (FileNotFoundError, OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    if help_result.returncode != 0 or feature_result.returncode != 0:
        return None
    if "--disable <FEATURE>" not in f"{help_result.stdout or ''}\n{help_result.stderr or ''}":
        return None
    if not _output_within_limit(help_result) or not _output_within_limit(feature_result):
        return None
    advertised = {
        line.split()[0] for line in (feature_result.stdout or "").splitlines() if line.split()
    }
    if any(feature not in advertised for feature in TOOL_POLICY_FEATURES):
        return None
    flags: list[str] = []
    for feature in TOOL_POLICY_FEATURES:
        flags.extend(("--disable", feature))
    return flags


def resolve_codex_cli() -> str:
    """Prefer the first PATH entry whose installed Codex launcher is runnable."""

    candidates = [
        str(path)
        for path in sorted(
            (Path.home() / ".nvm" / "versions" / "node").glob("*/bin/codex"),
            reverse=True,
        )
        if path.exists()
    ]
    resolved = shutil.which(CODEX)
    if resolved:
        candidates.append(resolved)
    candidates.extend(
        str(path)
        for path in (
            Path.home() / ".nvm" / "current" / "bin" / CODEX,
            Path.home() / ".local" / "bin" / CODEX,
        )
        if path.exists()
    )
    return next(iter(dict.fromkeys(candidates)), CODEX)


def _codex_executable(runner: Runner | None) -> str:
    return CODEX if runner is not None else resolve_codex_cli()


def _run(runner: Runner | None) -> Runner:
    return subprocess.run if runner is None else runner


def _print_json(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    print(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


def _status_payload(
    *,
    available: bool,
    authenticated: bool,
    auth_method: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "1.0.0",
        "command": "codex login status",
        "codex_cli_available": available,
        "authenticated": authenticated,
        "auth_method": auth_method,
        "oauth_prompt_ready": authenticated and auth_method == "chatgpt_oauth",
        "secrets_included": False,
    }


def query_auth_status(*, runner: Runner | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a minimal status projection without forwarding CLI output."""

    try:
        completed = _run(runner)(
            [_codex_executable(runner), "login", "status"],
            check=False,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=20,
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return _status_payload(
            available=False,
            authenticated=False,
            auth_method="unavailable",
        )
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return _status_payload(
            available=True,
            authenticated=False,
            auth_method="indeterminate",
        )

    if completed.returncode != 0:
        return _status_payload(
            available=True,
            authenticated=False,
            auth_method="none",
        )

    if not _output_within_limit(completed):
        return _status_payload(
            available=True,
            authenticated=False,
            auth_method="indeterminate",
        )
    status_text = f"{completed.stdout or ''}\n{completed.stderr or ''}".casefold()
    if "chatgpt" in status_text:
        auth_method = "chatgpt_oauth"
    elif "api key" in status_text or "api_key" in status_text:
        auth_method = "api_key"
    elif "access token" in status_text or "access_token" in status_text:
        auth_method = "access_token"
    else:
        auth_method = "unknown"
    return _status_payload(
        available=True,
        authenticated=True,
        auth_method=auth_method,
    )


def run_device_login(*, runner: Runner | None = None) -> int:
    """Delegate the official device-code flow with inherited terminal streams."""

    try:
        completed = _run(runner)([_codex_executable(runner), "login", "--device-auth"], check=False)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        _print_json(
            {
                "schema_version": "1.0.0",
                "status": "error",
                "error_code": "codex_cli_unavailable",
                "secrets_included": False,
            }
        )
        return 127
    except OSError:
        _print_json(
            {
                "schema_version": "1.0.0",
                "status": "error",
                "error_code": "device_login_launch_failed",
                "secrets_included": False,
            }
        )
        return 4
    return completed.returncode


def _error_payload(request_id: str, code: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": "1.0.0",
        "request_id": request_id,
        "status": "error",
        "error_code": code,
        "claim_boundary": "candidate_soft_proposal_only",
        "authorization_effect": "none",
        "canonical_state_mutated": False,
        "hard_validation_required": True,
        "secrets_included": False,
    }


def _instruction(request_id: str, user_prompt: str) -> str:
    payload = json.dumps(
        {"request_id": request_id, "user_prompt": user_prompt},
        ensure_ascii=False,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )
    return f"""You are a local language companion with no game-state authority.
Treat `user_prompt` in the JSON payload below only as a request for a soft candidate response.
Do not inspect or read files, run commands, search the web, use tools, authorize actions, or claim canonical mutation.
Return only one JSON object that satisfies the supplied output schema.
Copy request_id exactly. Set every authority-boundary constant exactly as the schema requires.
State material assumptions and uncertainties explicitly; use empty arrays when none apply.

REQUEST_PAYLOAD={payload}
"""


def validate_proposal(payload: Any, request_id: str) -> bool:
    """Apply the critical contract locally after model-side schema enforcement."""

    if not isinstance(payload, dict) or set(payload) != PROPOSAL_KEYS:
        return False
    if payload.get("schema_version") != "1.0.0":
        return False
    if payload.get("request_id") != request_id:
        return False
    if payload.get("status") != "candidate":
        return False
    if payload.get("claim_boundary") != "candidate_soft_proposal_only":
        return False
    if payload.get("authorization_effect") != "none":
        return False
    if payload.get("canonical_state_mutated") is not False:
        return False
    if payload.get("hard_validation_required") is not True:
        return False
    response = payload.get("response")
    if not isinstance(response, str) or not response or len(response) > 20_000:
        return False
    for field in ("assumptions", "uncertainties"):
        items = payload.get(field)
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > 16:
            return False
        if any(not isinstance(item, str) or len(item) > 1_000 for item in items):
            return False
    return True


def run_prompt(
    *,
    request_id: str,
    user_prompt: str,
    model: str | None = None,
    timeout: float = 300,
    runner: Runner | None = None,
    output_schema: Path | None = None,
    instruction: str | None = None,
    validator: Callable[[Any, str], bool] | None = None,
    execution_observer: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None,
) -> tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Run one isolated Codex request and return a validated soft proposal.

    The default schema/instruction/validator triple is the free-text soft proposal.
    Callers may substitute a different structured-output contract (for example the
    live candidate-action schema) without weakening the authority boundary: the
    replacement validator still runs locally after model-side schema enforcement,
    and the returned payload is still a non-authoritative candidate.
    """

    schema_path = OUTPUT_SCHEMA if output_schema is None else output_schema
    validate = validate_proposal if validator is None else validator
    if not REQUEST_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(request_id):
        return 2, _error_payload(request_id, "invalid_request_id")
    if not user_prompt.strip():
        return 2, _error_payload(request_id, "empty_prompt")
    if not schema_path.is_file():
        return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "output_schema_missing")
    if (
        not isinstance(timeout, (int, float))
        or isinstance(timeout, bool)
        or not 0 < timeout <= 3_600
    ):
        return 2, _error_payload(request_id, "invalid_timeout")

    auth = query_auth_status(runner=runner)
    if not auth["oauth_prompt_ready"]:
        return 3, _error_payload(request_id, "chatgpt_oauth_not_authenticated")
    policy_flags = _tool_policy_flags(runner)
    if policy_flags is None:
        return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_tool_policy_unsupported")

    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="trace-rpg-codex-oauth-") as directory:
            isolated_root = Path(directory).resolve()
            if isolated_root == PROJECT_ROOT or PROJECT_ROOT in isolated_root.parents:
                return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "temporary_workspace_not_isolated")
            output_path = isolated_root / "last-message.json"
            command = [
                _codex_executable(runner),
                "exec",
                *policy_flags,
                "--ephemeral",
                "--sandbox",
                "read-only",
                "--skip-git-repo-check",
                "--ignore-user-config",
                "--ignore-rules",
                "--output-schema",
                str(schema_path),
                "--output-last-message",
                str(output_path),
                "--color",
                "never",
                "-C",
                str(isolated_root),
            ]
            if model:
                command.extend(["--model", model])
            command.append("-")
            if execution_observer is not None:
                execution_observer(
                    {
                        "schema_version": "codex-exec-attempt/1",
                        "request_id": request_id,
                        "operation": "exec",
                        "executable": Path(command[0]).name,
                        "runner_injected": runner is not None,
                        "tool_policy_enforced": runner is None,
                        "disabled_feature_count": len(policy_flags) // 2,
                        "timeout_seconds": timeout,
                    }
                )
            completed = _run(runner)(
                command,
                input=(
                    _instruction(request_id, user_prompt) if instruction is None else instruction
                ),
                cwd=str(isolated_root),
                check=False,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=timeout,
            )
            if not _output_within_limit(completed):
                return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_exec_output_limit")
            if completed.returncode != 0:
                return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_exec_failed")
            try:
                if output_path.stat().st_size > MAX_FINAL_MESSAGE_BYTES:
                    return 5, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_output_limit")
                with output_path.open("rb") as handle:
                    raw_output = handle.read(MAX_FINAL_MESSAGE_BYTES + 1)
                if len(raw_output) > MAX_FINAL_MESSAGE_BYTES:
                    return 5, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_output_limit")
                proposal = json.loads(raw_output.decode("utf-8"))
            except (OSError, UnicodeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
                return 5, _error_payload(request_id, "invalid_codex_output")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return 127, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_cli_unavailable")
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_exec_timeout")
    except OSError:
        return 4, _error_payload(request_id, "codex_exec_launch_failed")

    if not validate(proposal, request_id):
        return 5, _error_payload(request_id, "soft_proposal_contract_failed")
    return 0, proposal


def _positive_int(value: str) -> int:
    number = int(value)
    if number < 1 or number > 3_600:
        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("timeout must be between 1 and 3600 seconds")
    return number


def parse_args(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    subparsers.add_parser("login", help="Run `codex login --device-auth` on the inherited TTY.")
    subparsers.add_parser("status", help="Print a secret-free JSON authentication status.")

    prompt = subparsers.add_parser("prompt", help="Request one isolated soft proposal.")
    prompt.add_argument("--request-id", required=True)
    prompt.add_argument("--model")
    prompt.add_argument("--timeout", type=_positive_int, default=300)
    prompt.add_argument("text", help="Prompt text; quote it when it contains spaces.")

    smoke = subparsers.add_parser("smoke", help="Run an explicit, quota-using live smoke request.")
    smoke.add_argument("--request-id", required=True)
    smoke.add_argument("--model")
    smoke.add_argument("--timeout", type=_positive_int, default=300)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "login":
        return run_device_login()
    if args.command == "status":
        payload = query_auth_status()
        _print_json(payload)
        return 0 if payload["oauth_prompt_ready"] else 3

    user_prompt = args.text if args.command == "prompt" else SMOKE_PROMPT
    code, payload = run_prompt(
        request_id=args.request_id,
        user_prompt=user_prompt,
        model=args.model,
        timeout=args.timeout,
    )
    _print_json(payload)
    return code


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

    "godot_failed",
    "godot_unavailable",
}
REPLAY_REPORT_KEYS = {"schema_version", "packet_id", "model_calls", "denominators", "rows"}
RUNTIME_SOURCE_PATHS = (
    "src/nesy_game/npc_goal.py",
    "scripts/codex_oauth_llm.py",
    "scripts/run_npc_goal_experiment.py",
    "game-track/godot/scripts/game3d/game_3d.gd",
    "game-track/godot/scripts/game3d/npc_agent_client.gd",
    "game-track/godot/scripts/sealed_lighthouse_machine.gd",
    "game-track/godot/scripts/canonical_state.gd",
    "game-track/godot/data/npc-goal-recovery.json",
    "game-track/godot/data/sealed_lighthouse.json",
    "game-track/godot/scenes/main_3d.tscn",
)


def canonical(value: Any) -> bytes:
    """Return the protocol's canonical JSON bytes (for value digests only)."""
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode(
        "utf-8"
    )


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical(value)).hexdigest()


def read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def runtime_source_hashes(root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Return hashes for every runtime source frozen by the experiment protocol."""
    return {path: sha256_file(root / path) for path in RUNTIME_SOURCE_PATHS}


def load_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for line_number, line in enumerate(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines(), 1):
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        try:
            value = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: invalid JSON: {exc}") from exc
        if not isinstance(value, dict):
            raise ProtocolError(f"{path}:{line_number}: row must be an object")
        rows.append(value)
    return rows


def _normalize_policy(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Mirror Godot's disclosure-policy integer normalization before hashing."""
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {key: _normalize_policy(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_normalize_policy(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        return int(value)
    return value


def policy_hash(root: Path) -> str:
    """Hash the canonical disclosure policy, never project.godot."""
    scenario_path = root / "game-track/godot/data/sealed_lighthouse.json"
    scenario = read_json(scenario_path)
    if not isinstance(scenario, Mapping) or not isinstance(
        scenario.get("disclosure_policy"), Mapping
    ):
        raise ProtocolError(f"disclosure_policy missing: {scenario_path}")
    return digest(_normalize_policy(scenario["disclosure_policy"]))


def config_hash(root: Path) -> str:
    """Hash the exact canonical config file bytes required by protocol 1.1."""
    return sha256_file(root / "configs/npc-goal-recovery.json")


def source_project_config_hash(root: Path) -> str:
    return sha256_file(root / "game-track/godot/project.godot")


def _is_sha(value: Any) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or len(value) != 64:
        return False
    try:
        int(value, 16)
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return value == value.lower()


def _project_relative(root: Path, value: Any) -> str | None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or Path(value).is_absolute():
        return None
    candidate = Path(value)
    if any(part == ".." for part in candidate.parts):
        return None
    try:
        resolved = (root / candidate).resolve()
        resolved.relative_to(root.resolve())
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return candidate.as_posix()


def _path(root: Path, packet_dir: Path | None, name: str) -> Path:
    return (
        packet_dir or root / "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"
    ) / name


def _fixture_path(root: Path, fixture_ref: Any) -> tuple[Path, str] | None:
    if not isinstance(fixture_ref, str) or "#" not in fixture_ref:
        return None
    path_text, case_id = fixture_ref.split("#", 1)
    relative = _project_relative(root, path_text)
    if relative is None:
        return None
    if not relative.startswith("data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/"):
        return None
    fixture_path = root / relative
    if not case_id:
        return None
    return fixture_path, case_id


def fixture_case(assignment: Mapping[str, Any], root: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Load and normalize one source fixture case without retaining raw text."""
    root = root or ROOT
    resolved = _fixture_path(root, assignment.get("fixture_ref"))
    if resolved is None:
        raise ValueError("fixture_ref must be a project-relative path plus #case id")
    fixture_path, case_id = resolved
    if not fixture_path.is_file():
        raise ValueError(f"fixture missing: {fixture_path}")
    payload = read_json(fixture_path)
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping) or not isinstance(payload.get("cases"), list):
        raise ProtocolError(f"invalid fixture packet: {fixture_path}")
    source = next(
        (
            case
            for case in payload["cases"]
            if isinstance(case, Mapping) and str(case.get("id")) == case_id
        ),
        None,
    )
    if not isinstance(source, Mapping):
        raise ProtocolError(f"fixture case missing: {assignment.get('fixture_ref')}")
    case = copy.deepcopy(dict(source))
    utterance = case.pop("utterance", None)
    if not isinstance(utterance, str) or len(utterance) > 1000:
        raise ValueError(f"invalid fixture utterance: {assignment.get('fixture_ref')}")
    utterance_hash = hashlib.sha256(utterance.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    if case.get("raw_utterance_sha256", utterance_hash) != utterance_hash:
        raise ValueError(f"fixture utterance hash mismatch: {assignment.get('fixture_ref')}")
    case["raw_utterance_sha256"] = utterance_hash
    source_kind = case.get("source_kind")
    if not isinstance(source_kind, str) or not source_kind:
        raise ValueError(f"fixture source_kind missing: {assignment.get('fixture_ref')}")
    observation = case.get("observation")
    if (
        not isinstance(observation, Mapping)
        or set(observation) != OBSERVATION_KEYS
        or any(type(v) is not bool for v in observation.values())
    ):
        raise ValueError(f"invalid fixture observation: {assignment.get('fixture_ref')}")
    stable_ref = f"{fixture_path.relative_to(root).as_posix()}#{case_id}"
    original_ref = str(assignment.get("fixture_ref"))
    if original_ref != stable_ref:
        raise ValueError(f"fixture_ref is not canonical project-relative path: {original_ref}")
    case["fixture_ref"] = stable_ref
    case["fixture_ref_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(stable_ref.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    return case


def assignment_key(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return digest({key: value for key, value in row.items() if key != "assignment_key"})


def persisted_has_raw_utterance(value: Any) -> bool:
    """Return true only when persisted data contains actual utterance text.

    Replay envelopes retain the literal ``[REDACTED]`` sentinel (and may retain
    ``null`` for non-request rows); neither is raw input.  Any other value under
    an ``utterance`` key is a provenance violation.
    """
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, item in value.items():
            if key == "utterance":
                if item is not None and item != "[REDACTED]":
                    return True
            elif persisted_has_raw_utterance(item):
                return True
        return False
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return any(persisted_has_raw_utterance(item) for item in value)
    return False


def _normalize_numbers(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Normalize Godot's JSON numeric floats for semantic record comparisons."""
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {key: _normalize_numbers(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_normalize_numbers(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, float) and value.is_integer():
        return int(value)
    return value


def _semantic_equal(left: Any, right: Any) -> bool:
    return _normalize_numbers(left) == _normalize_numbers(right)


def _engine_action_signature(action: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    after = action.get("state_ref_after")
    before = action.get("state_ref_before")
    observation = action.get("observation_after", action.get("observation"))
    return {
        "action_id": str(action.get("action_id", "")),
        "operation": str(action.get("operation", "")),
        "execution_mode": str(action.get("execution_mode", "")),
        "arguments": copy.deepcopy(action.get("arguments", {})),
        "status": str(action.get("status", "")),
        "observation": copy.deepcopy(observation),
        "state_ref_before": copy.deepcopy(before),
        "state_ref_after": copy.deepcopy(after),
        "engine_revision": after.get("revision") if isinstance(after, Mapping) else None,
        "state_hash_after": after.get("sha256") if isinstance(after, Mapping) else None,
    }


def _engine_reexecution_comparison(
    original: Mapping[str, Any], replayed: Mapping[str, Any]
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    def actions(report: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = report.get("actions", report.get("rows", []))
        return [_engine_action_signature(row) for row in rows] if isinstance(rows, list) else []

    comparison = {
        "terminal_status": original.get("status", original.get("terminal_status"))
        == replayed.get("status", replayed.get("terminal_status")),
        "initial_state_ref": original.get("initial_state_ref") == replayed.get("initial_state_ref"),
        "final_state_ref": original.get("final_state_ref") == replayed.get("final_state_ref"),
        "decision_sha256": original.get("decision_sha256") == replayed.get("decision_sha256"),
        "actions": actions(original) == actions(replayed),
        "expected_negative_gates": original.get("expected_negative_gates", [])
        == replayed.get("expected_negative_gates", []),
    }
    comparison["passed"] = all(comparison.values())
    return comparison


def check_budget(actual: Any, expected: Mapping[str, Any], where: str, errors: list[str]) -> None:
    if not isinstance(actual, Mapping) or set(actual) != PROTOCOL_BUDGET_KEYS:
        errors.append(f"{where}: budget keys/shape mismatch")
        return
    if dict(actual) != dict(expected):
        errors.append(f"{where}: budget differs from frozen config")
    for key in ("input_tokens", "output_tokens", "cost"):
        if actual[key] is not None and (
            not isinstance(actual[key], (int, float)) or actual[key] < 0
        ):
            errors.append(f"{where}: invalid {key}")


def _state_ref(value: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, Mapping)
        and set(value) == {"revision", "sha256"}
        and type(value["revision"]) is int
        and value["revision"] >= 0
        and _is_sha(value["sha256"])
    )


def _observation(value: Any) -> bool:
    return (
        isinstance(value, Mapping)
        and set(value) == OBSERVATION_KEYS
        and all(type(value[key]) is bool for key in OBSERVATION_KEYS)
    )


def _snapshot_ref(report: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    for key in ("actual_state_ref", "initial_state_ref", "state_ref", "state_ref_before"):
        value = report.get(key)
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            return value
    snapshot = report.get("initial_public_snapshot")
    if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) and isinstance(snapshot.get("state_ref"), Mapping):
        return snapshot["state_ref"]
    return None


def _engine_action_rows(report: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    rows = report.get("actions", report.get("rows", []))
    return [row for row in rows if isinstance(row, Mapping)] if isinstance(rows, list) else []


def _validate_action_transition(action: Mapping[str, Any], where: str, errors: list[str]) -> None:
    before = action.get("state_ref_before")
    after = action.get("state_ref_after", action.get("state_ref"))
    before_obs = action.get("observation_before")
    after_obs = action.get("observation_after", action.get("observation"))
    operation = str(action.get("operation", ""))
    status = str(action.get("status", ""))
    if not _state_ref(before) or not _state_ref(after):
        errors.append(f"{where}: action state refs missing or malformed")
        return
    if not _observation(before_obs) or not _observation(after_obs):
        errors.append(f"{where}: action observations missing or malformed")
        return
    # Native action rows do not carry engine_revision; result envelopes do.
    if "engine_revision" in action and action.get("engine_revision") != after["revision"]:
        errors.append(f"{where}: engine revision mismatch")
    same_state = dict(before) == dict(after)
    same_observation = dict(before_obs) == dict(after_obs)
    accepted = (
        action.get("result", {}).get("accepted")
        if isinstance(action.get("result"), Mapping)
        else None
    )
    if status == "APPLIED":
        if accepted is not True:
            errors.append(f"{where}: APPLIED action is not accepted")
        if operation in WORLD_OPERATIONS:
            if after["revision"] != before["revision"] + 1 or after["sha256"] == before["sha256"]:
                errors.append(
                    f"{where}: world APPLIED transition must increment revision and change hash"
                )
        elif (
            operation in DIALOGUE_OPERATIONS
            or action.get("execution_mode") == "dialogue"
            or operation in {"no_route", "no-route"}
        ):
            if not same_state or not same_observation:
                errors.append(f"{where}: dialogue/no-route APPLIED transition must be unchanged")
        elif not same_state or not same_observation:
            errors.append(f"{where}: unknown APPLIED operation changed canonical state")
    elif status in NON_APPLIED or status:
        if accepted is True:
            errors.append(f"{where}: non-applied action marked accepted")
        if not same_state or not same_observation:
            errors.append(f"{where}: non-applied transition changed state or observation")
    else:
        errors.append(f"{where}: action status missing")


def _validate_negative_gates(
    report: Mapping[str, Any],
    where: str,
    errors: list[str],
    required_controls: list[str] | None = None,
) -> None:
    gates = report.get("expected_negative_gates")
    required = list(required_controls or [])
    if gates is None:
        gates = []
    if not isinstance(gates, list):
        errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate list malformed")
        return
    if not required and gates:
        errors.append(f"{where}: negative gates declared without canonical controls")
    controls = [gate.get("control") if isinstance(gate, Mapping) else None for gate in gates]
    if required and controls != required:
        errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate coverage mismatch")
    for index, gate in enumerate(gates):
        if not isinstance(gate, Mapping) or gate.get("passed") is not True:
            errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate[{index}] failed")
            continue
        before, after = gate.get("state_ref_before"), gate.get("state_ref_after")
        if not _state_ref(before) or not _state_ref(after) or dict(before) != dict(after):
            errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate[{index}] changed canonical state")
        expected = str(gate.get("expected_status", ""))
        observed = str(gate.get("observed_status", ""))
        control = str(gate.get("control", ""))
        status_ok = observed == expected
        if control == "stale_revision" and expected == "STALE" and observed == "REJECTED":
            status_ok = True
        if control == "no_route":
            proof = (
                gate.get("physical_route_unreachable") is True and gate.get("no_world_step") is True
            )
            status_ok = status_ok or (
                expected == "NO_CANONICAL_ROUTE" and observed in {"NO_CANONICAL_ROUTE", "NO_ROUTE"}
            )
            if observed == "APPLIED":
                status_ok = expected == "NO_CANONICAL_ROUTE" and proof
            if not proof:
                errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate[{index}] no-route proof missing")
        if control == "cancelled" and expected == "CANCELLED" and observed == "ERROR":
            codes = gate.get("codes")
            status_ok = isinstance(codes, list) and {str(code) for code in codes} >= {
                "EPOCH_MISMATCH",
                "GENERATION_MISMATCH",
            }
        if expected and not status_ok:
            errors.append(f"{where}: negative gate[{index}] status mismatch")


def _artifact_path(
    root: Path, packet_dir: Path, value: Any, artifact_root: Path | None = None
) -> Path | None:
    """Resolve a recorded artifact reference to one member of the current packet.

    Relative references are packet-local.  Native commands may retain an absolute
    path from the recorded ``artifact_root``; that path is admitted only when it
    names a member below that declared root, then is rebased to the current packet
    directory.  Both forms pass through the same resolved containment check so
    relocation cannot turn a basename or outsider path into a valid artifact.
    """
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
        return None
    packet = packet_dir.resolve()
    candidate = Path(value)
    try:
        if candidate.is_absolute():
            if artifact_root is None:
                return None
            recorded_root = artifact_root.resolve()
            recorded_path = candidate.resolve()
            relative = recorded_path.relative_to(recorded_root)
            path = (packet / relative).resolve()
        else:
            path = (packet / candidate).resolve()
        path.relative_to(packet)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    return path if path.is_file() else None


def _artifact_exists(
    root: Path, value: Any, packet_dir: Path | None = None, artifact_root: Path | None = None
) -> bool:
    if packet_dir is None:
        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
            return False
        candidate = Path(value)
        return (candidate if candidate.is_absolute() else root / candidate).is_file()
    return _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, value, artifact_root) is not None


def _sidecar_json(
    root: Path, packet_dir: Path, value: Any, artifact_root: Path | None = None
) -> Mapping[str, Any] | None:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value or Path(value).is_absolute():
        return None
    path = _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, value, artifact_root)
    if path is None:
        return None
    try:
        payload = read_json(path)
    except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return None
    return payload if isinstance(payload, Mapping) else None


def _validate_input_file_hash(
    root: Path,
    packet_dir: Path,
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
    where: str,
    errors: list[str],
    artifact_root: Path | None = None,
) -> None:
    input_value = metadata.get("input_path")
    if not isinstance(input_value, str) or not input_value or Path(input_value).is_absolute():
        errors.append(f"{where}: native input_path must be packet-relative")
        return
    input_path = _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, input_value, artifact_root)
    if input_path is None:
        errors.append(f"{where}: native input artifact missing or outside packet")
        return
    actual = sha256_file(input_path)
    if (
        metadata.get("input_sha256_before") != actual
        or metadata.get("input_sha256_after") != actual
    ):
        errors.append(f"{where}: native input SHA does not match actual immutable file")
    if metadata.get("input_bytes_preserved") is not True:
        errors.append(f"{where}: native input bytes were not preserved")


def _command_input_path(
    root: Path, packet_dir: Path, command: Any, artifact_root: Path | None
) -> Path | None:
    if not isinstance(command, list):
        return None
    for index, token in enumerate(command[:-1]):
        if isinstance(token, str) and (
            token == "--npc-bootstrap" or token == "--npc-experiment" or token == "--npc-mode"
        ):
            return _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, command[index + 1], artifact_root)
    return None


def _stage_files(
    root: Path,
    packet_dir: Path,
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
    stage: str,
    where: str,
    errors: list[str],
    artifact_root: Path | None,
) -> tuple[Path | None, Path | None, Mapping[str, Any] | None, Any]:
    _validate_input_file_hash(root, packet_dir, metadata, where, errors, artifact_root)
    input_value = metadata.get("input_path")
    input_path = (
        _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, input_value, artifact_root)
        if isinstance(input_value, str) and not Path(input_value).is_absolute()
        else None
    )
    command_path = _command_input_path(root, packet_dir, metadata.get("command"), artifact_root)
    if input_path is None:
        errors.append(f"{where}: input artifact missing or outside packet")
    if command_path is None or input_path is None or command_path != input_path:
        errors.append(f"{where}: command input does not match metadata.input_path")
    sidecar_value = metadata.get("sidecar_path")
    sidecar_path = (
        _artifact_path(root, packet_dir, sidecar_value, artifact_root)
        if isinstance(sidecar_value, str) and not Path(sidecar_value).is_absolute()
        else None
    )
    if sidecar_path is None:
        errors.append(f"{where}: sidecar artifact missing or outside packet")
    elif input_path is not None:
        expected_suffix = ".bootstrap.json" if stage == "bootstrap" else ".result.json"
        if sidecar_path.name != input_path.name + expected_suffix:
            errors.append(f"{where}: sidecar_path has wrong suffix")
    sidecar = _sidecar_json(root, packet_dir, sidecar_value, artifact_root)
    if sidecar is None:
        errors.append(f"{where}: sidecar artifact missing or unreadable")
    embedded = metadata.get("trace")
    if not isinstance(embedded, Mapping):
        errors.append(f"{where}: embedded trace missing")
    elif sidecar is not None and sidecar != embedded:
        errors.append(f"{where}: sidecar does not match embedded trace")
    payload: Any = None
    if input_path is not None:
        try:
            payload = read_json(input_path)
        except (OSError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
            errors.append(f"{where}: input JSON unreadable: {exc}")
    return input_path, sidecar_path, sidecar, payload


def _required_negative_controls(case: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    mutations = case.get("mutations", [])
    return [str(item) for item in mutations] if isinstance(mutations, list) else []


def _validate_native_input(
    payload: Any,
    assignment: Mapping[str, Any],
    record: Mapping[str, Any],
    case: Mapping[str, Any],
    stage: str,
    where: str,
    errors: list[str],
    expected_hashes: Mapping[str, Any],
    check_decision: bool = True,
) -> None:
    if not isinstance(payload, Mapping):
        errors.append(f"{where}: native input must be an object")
        return
    for key in ("assignment_id", "fixture_ref", "fixture_input_sha256", "config_hash"):
        if payload.get(key) != assignment.get(key):
            errors.append(f"{where}: input {key} does not bind assignment")
    if payload.get("source_hashes") != dict(expected_hashes):
        errors.append(f"{where}: input source hashes mismatch")
    if payload.get("setup_operations") != case.get("setup_operations", []):
        errors.append(f"{where}: input setup recipe mismatch")
    if payload.get("seed_world_events") != case.get("seed_world_events", []):
        errors.append(f"{where}: input seed recipe mismatch")
    if stage == "bootstrap":
        if payload.get("mutations") != case.get("mutations", []):
