        aggregate_path = args.output_dir / (
            "engineering-aggregate.json" if packet_id == "NPC-ENG1" else "aggregate.json"
        )
        packet_aggregate = aggregate(
            records,
            assignments,
            packet_id=packet_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
            npc_protocol_config_sha256=npc_protocol_config_sha256,
            source_schema_sha256=source_schema_sha256,
            source_project_config_sha256=source_project_config_sha256,
            policy_hash=policy_hash,
        )
        packet_aggregate["run_mode"] = run_mode
        packet_aggregate["cli_exec_attempt_count"] = sum(row["model_calls"] for row in records)
        write_json(aggregate_path, packet_aggregate)
        all_records.extend(records)
    source_project_config_sha256_after = source_project_config_hash(ROOT)
    source_manifest = {
        "schema_version": PACKET_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "config_hash": config_hash,
        "source_project_config_sha256": source_project_config_sha256,
        "source_project_config_sha256_after": source_project_config_sha256_after,
        "source_project_unchanged": source_project_config_sha256_after
        == source_project_config_sha256,
        "godot_import": godot_import,
        "staged_godot_project": str(staged_godot_project) if staged_godot_project else None,
        "npc_protocol_config_sha256": npc_protocol_config_sha256,
        "source_schema_sha256": source_schema_sha256,
        "policy_hash": policy_hash,
        "packets": [packet_id for packet_id, *_ in packets],
        "counts": {packet_id: expected for packet_id, _cases, _path, expected in packets},
        "redaction": "raw utterances are transient; persisted records retain fixture and utterance digests only",
    }
    source_manifest["policy_admission"] = policy_engine
    source_manifest["admission_failure"] = (
        dict(admission_failure) if admission_failure is not None else None
    )
    source_manifest["run_mode"] = run_mode
    source_manifest["cli_exec_attempt_count"] = sum(row["model_calls"] for row in all_records)
    source_manifest["artifact_root"] = str(args.output_dir)
    source_manifest["runtime_source_hashes_before"] = runtime_source_hashes_before
    source_manifest["runtime_source_hashes_after"] = {
        path: sha256_file(ROOT / path) for path in runtime_source_hashes_before
    }
    source_manifest["runtime_sources_unchanged"] = (
        source_manifest["runtime_source_hashes_after"] == runtime_source_hashes_before
    )
    write_json(args.output_dir / "source-manifest.json", source_manifest)
    if all_records and not args.dry_run:
        write_json(
            args.output_dir / "replay-results.json",
            replay_report(
                all_records,
                "NPC-SCR1"
                if args.packet == "screening"
                else "NPC-ENG1"
                if args.packet == "engineering"
                else "NPC-ALL",
            ),
        )
        write_sha256_manifest(args.output_dir)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "ok",
                "packets": [packet_id for packet_id, *_ in packets],
                "records": len(all_records),
                "output_dir": str(args.output_dir),
                "files": sorted(path.name for path in args.output_dir.iterdir() if path.is_file()),
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    )
    return (
        0
        if source_manifest["runtime_sources_unchanged"]
        and source_manifest["source_project_unchanged"]
        else 1
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Write the deterministic editable-source manifest for tables and figures."""

PLAYABLE_READOUT_SET = "paper/latex/captures/playable-readout-20261014-r2"


def receipt(relative_path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = ROOT / relative_path
    payload = path.read_bytes()
    return {
        "path": relative_path,
        "bytes": len(payload),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest(),
    }


def receipts(paths: Iterable[str]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [receipt(path) for path in paths]


def visual_asset(
    asset_id: str,
    kind: str,
    *,
    rendered: tuple[str, ...],
    editable: tuple[str, ...],
    generator: str,
    data: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    used_by: tuple[str, ...] = (),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": asset_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "rendered": receipts(rendered),
        "editable_sources": receipts(editable),
        "generator": receipt(generator),
        "data_sources": receipts(data),
        "used_by": list(used_by),
    }


def table_source(
    source_id: str,
    *,
    layouts: tuple[str, ...],
    generator: str | None,
    data: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    readable: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    rendered_in: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    anchors: tuple[str, ...] = (),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": source_id,
        "kind": "table",
        "layout_sources": receipts(layouts),
        "generator": receipt(generator) if generator else None,
        "data_sources": receipts(data),
        "readable_sources": receipts(readable),
        "rendered_in": list(rendered_in),
        "anchors": list(anchors),
    }


def build_manifest() -> dict[str, Any]:
    paper_usage = ("paper/latex/en/main.tex", "paper/latex/ko/main.tex")
    direction_note = ("research/directions/consensus-vs-symbolic-gate.md",)

    paper_assets = []
    for stem, asset_id, kind, data in (
        ("fig_architecture", "paper-architecture", "diagram", ()),
        (
            "fig_evidence_regimes",
            "paper-evidence-regimes",
            "chart",
            (
                "research/academic-pipeline/stage-04-pilot/pilot-results.json",
                "research/academic-pipeline/rq2-live-pilot/signal-repair-v2/policy_blind/summary.json",
                "research/academic-pipeline/rq2-live-pilot/frozen-pilot-base/goal_directed_blind/summary.json",
            ),
        ),
    ):
        base = f"paper/latex/figures/{stem}"
        paper_assets.append(
            visual_asset(
                asset_id,
                kind,
                rendered=(f"{base}.pdf", f"{base}.png"),
                editable=(f"{base}.svg",),
                generator=PAPER_FIGURE_GENERATOR,
                data=data,
                used_by=paper_usage,
            )
        )
    paper_assets.append(
        visual_asset(
            "paper-npc-screening",

            rendered=(
                "paper/latex/figures/fig_npc_screening.pdf",
                "paper/latex/figures/fig_npc_screening.png",

                "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004/screening-assignment-manifest.json",

        visual_asset(
            "direction-consensus-gate-lanes",
            "diagram",

            editable=("research/directions/figures/fig_consensus_gate_lanes.svg",),
            generator=DIRECTION_GENERATOR,
            used_by=direction_note,
        ),
        visual_asset(
            "direction-cost-validity-pareto",
            "diagram",
            rendered=("research/directions/figures/fig_cost_validity_pareto_concept.svg",),
            editable=("research/directions/figures/fig_cost_validity_pareto_concept.svg",),
            generator=DIRECTION_GENERATOR,
            used_by=direction_note,
        ),
        visual_asset(
            "kg-ontology-evaluation-matrix",
            "table-chart",
            rendered=("research/simulation/kg-ontology/latest/figures/evaluation-matrix.svg",),
            editable=("research/simulation/kg-ontology/latest/figures/evaluation-matrix.svg",),
            generator=KG_GENERATOR,
            data=(
                "research/simulation/kg-ontology/latest/evaluation-matrix.json",
                "research/simulation/kg-ontology/latest/strategy-trials.tsv",
            ),
            used_by=(),
        ),
        visual_asset(
            "game-balance-archetypes",
            "chart",
            rendered=("game-track/godot/docs/latest/balance-archetypes.svg",),
            editable=("game-track/godot/docs/latest/balance-archetypes.svg",),

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "generated_by": "scripts/update_visual_source_manifest.py",
        "rendered_surfaces": rendered_surfaces,
        "visual_assets": paper_assets + adjacent_assets,
        "table_sources": table_sources,
        "noneditable_evidence": noneditable_evidence,
    }


def main() -> None:
    payload = json.dumps(build_manifest(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n"
    MANIFEST.write_text(payload, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    print(f"wrote {MANIFEST.relative_to(ROOT)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== tests/test_npc_goal_contract.py | sha256:8b0a186183e9b67f96e39f69cfd30939037d899897a0f5670e6c82cf8323abdc | bytes:20476 =====
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

P = Path(
    "/Users/jangyoung/orca/neural_symbolic_in_game/neuro-symbolic-interactive-game-research-2026"
)
sys.path.insert(0, str(P / "src"))

from nesy_game.npc_goal import (
    BudgetExceeded,
    ControllerFailure,
    GoalController,
    IntentInterpreter,
    ProtocolError,
    ReplayValidator,
    content_hash,
    default_envelope,
    sha256_json,
    validate_envelope,
    verify_claims,
)

POLICY_HASH = "2e7966bf2ac6b54bfaf2db84b3f446686436bd3a1f8efea592ffa82e91249edd"
OBS_KEYS = {
    "lens_reachable",
    "lens_carried",
    "signal_restored",
    "tide_lead_authorized",
    "goal_reached",
    "in_dialogue",
}


def observation(**overrides: bool) -> dict[str, bool]:
    value = {key: False for key in OBS_KEYS}
    value.update(overrides)
    return value


def request(
    *,
    utterance: str = "Where is the lens?",
    obs: dict[str, bool] | None = None,
    request_id: str = "request-1",
    epoch: int = 0,
    revision: int = 0,
) -> dict:
    return default_envelope(
        message_type="request",
        request_id=request_id,
        session_id="contract-session",
        session_epoch=epoch,
        state_ref={
            "revision": revision,
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(f"state-{revision}".encode()).hexdigest(),
        },
        body={
            "event_kind": "utterance",
            "utterance": utterance,
            "observation": obs or observation(lens_reachable=True),
        },
        arm="R",
        backend="rule",
        policy_hash=POLICY_HASH,
        sequence=-1,
        delivery_index=-1,
    )


class NpcGoalContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_fixed_goal_and_observation_driven_plan_table(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        cases = (
            (
                "reachable_not_carried",
                observation(lens_reachable=True),
                "WAITING_PLAYER",
                "acquire_object",
            ),
            (
                "carried_requires_install",
                observation(lens_carried=True),
                "WAITING_PLAYER",
                "install_lens",
            ),
            (
                "restored_unauthorized_no_route",
                observation(lens_carried=True, signal_restored=True),
                "NO_ROUTE",
                None,
            ),
            (
                "restored_authorized_needs_dialogue",
                observation(lens_carried=True, signal_restored=True, tide_lead_authorized=True),
                "WAITING_DIALOGUE",
                "negotiate",
            ),
            (
                "restored_authorized_dialogue_reveals",
                observation(
                    lens_carried=True,
                    signal_restored=True,
                    tide_lead_authorized=True,
                    in_dialogue=True,
                ),
                "READY",
                "reveal_hint",
            ),
            (
                "unreachable_not_carried_no_route",
                observation(lens_reachable=False),
                "NO_ROUTE",
                None,
            ),
            (
                "goal_precedes_intent",
                observation(goal_reached=True, in_dialogue=True),
                "GOAL_REACHED",
                None,
            ),
        )
        for index, (name, obs, status, operation) in enumerate(cases):
            with self.subTest(case=name):
                response = controller.handle_request(
                    request(request_id=f"request-{index}", obs=obs)
                )
                body = response["body"]
                self.assertEqual(body["status"], status)
                self.assertEqual(
                    body["steps"][0]["operation"] if body["steps"] else None, operation
                )
                self.assertEqual(
                    response["goal_id"], "harbor-signal-restored-authorized-tide-lead-v1"
                )
                self.assertEqual(response["goal_version"], "1")
                self.assertEqual(response["policy_hash"], POLICY_HASH)

    def test_refusal_and_ambiguity_are_legal_clarify_without_goal_change(self) -> None:
        cases = ("No, leave me alone.", "Please help.")
        for index, utterance in enumerate(cases):
            with self.subTest(utterance=utterance):
                response = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R")).handle_request(
                    request(
                        utterance=utterance,
                        obs=observation(
                            lens_carried=True,
                            signal_restored=True,
                            tide_lead_authorized=True,
                            in_dialogue=True,
                        ),
                        request_id=f"clarify-{index}",
                    )
                )
                self.assertEqual(response["body"]["status"], "WAITING_DIALOGUE")
                self.assertEqual(response["body"]["steps"][0]["operation"], "clarify")
                self.assertEqual(response["body"]["steps"][0]["execution_mode"], "dialogue")
                self.assertEqual(
                    response["goal_id"], "harbor-signal-restored-authorized-tide-lead-v1"
                )
                self.assertEqual(response["goal_version"], "1")

    def test_plan_expansion_budget_counts_public_search_nodes(self) -> None:
        budget = {
            "intent_deadline_ms": 30_000,
            "episode_deadline_ms": 900_000,
            "max_intent_calls": 1,
            "max_action_attempts": 32,
            "max_replans": 16,
            "max_plan_expansions": 2,
            "max_inflight_per_session": 1,
            "input_tokens": None,
            "output_tokens": None,
            "cost": None,
        }
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"), budget=budget)
        with self.assertRaises(BudgetExceeded) as raised:
            controller.handle_request(
                request(
                    request_id="expansion-budget", obs=observation(lens_reachable=True), epoch=0
                )
            )
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "max_plan_expansions")

    def test_replay_accepts_enabled_reveal_and_rejects_unsafe_reveal(self) -> None:
        authorized = observation(
            lens_carried=True, signal_restored=True, tide_lead_authorized=True, in_dialogue=True
        )
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        source = request(
            utterance="Tell me the clue.", obs=authorized, request_id="authorized-replay"
        )
        decision = controller.handle_request(source)
        records = [source, decision]
        before_structure = copy.deepcopy(records)
        before_bytes = json.dumps(
            records, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
        ).encode("utf-8")
        first = ReplayValidator.validate(records, expected_session_id=source["session_id"])
        self.assertTrue(first["valid"])
        self.assertEqual(records, before_structure)
        self.assertEqual(
            json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode(
                "utf-8"
            ),
            before_bytes,
        )
        second = ReplayValidator.validate(records, expected_session_id=source["session_id"])
        self.assertTrue(second["valid"])
        self.assertEqual(records, before_structure)
        self.assertEqual(
            json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode(
                "utf-8"
            ),
            before_bytes,
        )
        self.assertEqual(decision["body"]["steps"][0]["operation"], "reveal_hint")

        unsafe_source = request(
            utterance="Tell me the clue.",
            obs=observation(
                lens_carried=True,
                signal_restored=True,
                tide_lead_authorized=False,
                in_dialogue=True,
            ),
            request_id="unsafe-replay",
        )
        unsafe_decision = controller.handle_request(unsafe_source)
        self.assertEqual(unsafe_decision["body"]["status"], "NO_ROUTE")
        unsafe_decision["body"]["status"] = "READY"
        unsafe_decision["body"]["steps"] = [
            {
                "action_id": "unsafe-reveal",
                "operation": "reveal_hint",
                "execution_mode": "commit_world",
                "arguments": {"actor_id": "captain_mira", "fact_id": "tide_marks_hint"},
            }
        ]
        unsafe_decision["content_sha256"] = content_hash(unsafe_decision)
        with self.assertRaises(ProtocolError) as raised:
            ReplayValidator.validate(
                [unsafe_source, unsafe_decision], expected_session_id=unsafe_source["session_id"]
            )
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "replay_plan_semantics_mismatch")

    def test_replay_accepts_matching_request_and_decision_state_ref(self) -> None:
        source = request(
            utterance="Tell me the clue.",
            obs=observation(
                lens_carried=True,
                signal_restored=True,
                tide_lead_authorized=True,
                in_dialogue=True,
            ),
            request_id="matching-state-ref",
            revision=7,
        )
        decision = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R")).handle_request(source)

        replay = ReplayValidator.validate(
            [source, decision], expected_session_id=source["session_id"]
        )

        self.assertTrue(replay["valid"])

    def test_replay_rejects_resigned_decision_state_ref_mismatch(self) -> None:
        source = request(
            utterance="Tell me the clue.",
            obs=observation(
                lens_carried=True,
                signal_restored=True,
                tide_lead_authorized=True,
                in_dialogue=True,
            ),
            request_id="mismatched-state-ref",
            revision=7,
        )
        decision = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R")).handle_request(source)
        self.assertTrue(
            ReplayValidator.validate([source, decision], expected_session_id=source["session_id"])[
                "valid"
            ]
        )

        mismatches = (
            (
                "revision-differs",
                {
                    "revision": 8,
                    "sha256": hashlib.sha256(b"state-8").hexdigest(),
                },
            ),
            (
                "hash-differs",
                {
                    "revision": source["state_ref"]["revision"],
                    "sha256": "f" * 64,
                },
            ),
        )
        for case, state_ref in mismatches:
            with self.subTest(case=case):
                tampered = copy.deepcopy(decision)
                tampered["state_ref"] = state_ref
                tampered["content_sha256"] = content_hash(tampered)

                with self.assertRaises(ProtocolError) as raised:
                    ReplayValidator.validate(
                        [source, tampered], expected_session_id=source["session_id"]
                    )
                self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "replay_state_ref_mismatch")

    def test_claims_are_verified_against_public_observation_not_treated_as_facts(self) -> None:
        intent = {
            "label": "REPORT_FACT",
            "claims": [{"code": "SIGNAL_RESTORED", "state": "UNVERIFIED"}],
            "target": None,
        }
        checked = verify_claims(intent, observation(lens_carried=False, signal_restored=False))
        self.assertEqual(checked["claims"][0]["state"], "CONTRADICTED")
        self.assertEqual(checked["claims"][0]["code"], "SIGNAL_RESTORED")

    def test_codex_instruction_contains_public_utterance_only(self) -> None:
        seen: dict[str, str] = {}

        def runner(**kwargs):
            seen["instruction"] = kwargs["instruction"]
            return 0, {
                "request_id": kwargs["request_id"],
                "typed_intent": {"label": "ASK_INFORMATION", "claims": [], "target": None},
            }

        interpreter = IntentInterpreter(arm="P", prompt_runner=runner)
        result = interpreter.interpret(request_id="public-request", utterance="Can you help me?")
        self.assertEqual(result.typed_intent["label"], "ASK_INFORMATION")
        for forbidden in ("tide_marks_hint", "sealed_lighthouse", "canonical_state", "worldstate"):
            self.assertNotIn(forbidden, seen["instruction"])
        self.assertIn('"utterance":"Can you help me?"', seen["instruction"])

    def test_refusal_or_ambiguity_must_change_legal_plan_not_only_trace(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        base = observation(lens_reachable=True)
        ask = controller.handle_request(
            request(utterance="Where is the lens?", obs=base, request_id="ask")
        )
        refusal = controller.handle_request(
            request(utterance="No, leave me alone.", obs=base, request_id="refusal")
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(ask["body"]["steps"], refusal["body"]["steps"])
        self.assertEqual(refusal["body"]["status"], "WAITING_DIALOGUE")
        self.assertEqual(refusal["body"]["steps"][0]["operation"], "clarify")

    def test_same_request_is_deduplicated_without_new_action(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"), cache_ttl_seconds=60)
        first_request = request(request_id="same")
        first = controller.handle_request(first_request)
        second = controller.handle_request(first_request)
        self.assertEqual(first, second)
        self.assertEqual(first["body"]["steps"][0]["operation"], "acquire_object")

    def test_request_id_conflict_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        controller.handle_request(request(request_id="conflict", utterance="Where is the lens?"))
        with self.assertRaises(ProtocolError) as raised:
            controller.handle_request(request(request_id="conflict", utterance="Tell me the clue."))
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "request_id_conflict")

    def test_result_duplicate_has_no_second_action_attempt(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        decision = controller.handle_request(request(request_id="plan"))
        step = decision["body"]["steps"][0]
        returned_state_ref = {"revision": 1, "sha256": "1" * 64}
        result = default_envelope(
            message_type="result",
            session_id="contract-session",
            request_id="plan",
            state_ref=returned_state_ref,
            body={
                "action_id": step["action_id"],
                "operation": step["operation"],
                "status": "APPLIED",
                "engine_revision": returned_state_ref["revision"],
                "state_ref": returned_state_ref,
                "feedback": "applied",
                "observation": observation(lens_reachable=True),
            },
            policy_hash=POLICY_HASH,
        )
        accepted = controller.handle_result(result)
        duplicate = controller.handle_result(result)
        self.assertEqual(accepted["body"]["status"], "ACCEPTED")
        self.assertEqual(duplicate["body"]["status"], "DUPLICATE")

    def test_result_engine_revision_must_equal_returned_state_revision(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        source = request(request_id="revision-equality")
        decision = controller.handle_request(source)
        step = decision["body"]["steps"][0]
        result = default_envelope(
            message_type="result",
            session_id="contract-session",
            request_id="revision-equality",
            state_ref=source["state_ref"],
            body={
                "action_id": step["action_id"],
                "operation": step["operation"],
                "status": "APPLIED",
                "engine_revision": source["state_ref"]["revision"] + 1,
                "state_ref": source["state_ref"],
                "feedback": "applied",
                "observation": observation(lens_reachable=True),
            },
            policy_hash=POLICY_HASH,
        )
        rejected = controller.handle_result(result)
        self.assertEqual(rejected["body"]["status"], "REJECTED")
        self.assertEqual(rejected["body"]["error"], "contradictory_result")

    def test_call_budget_is_terminal_and_explicit(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(
            interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"),
            budget={
                "intent_deadline_ms": 30_000,
                "episode_deadline_ms": 900_000,
                "max_intent_calls": 1,
                "max_action_attempts": 32,
                "max_replans": 16,
                "max_plan_expansions": 32,
                "max_inflight_per_session": 1,
                "input_tokens": None,
                "output_tokens": None,
                "cost": None,
            },
        )
        controller.handle_request(request(request_id="budget-1"))
        with self.assertRaises(BudgetExceeded) as raised:
            controller.handle_request(request(request_id="budget-2"))
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "max_intent_calls")

    def test_trace_is_redacted_and_replay_tampering_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        controller = GoalController(interpreter=IntentInterpreter(arm="R"))
        controller.handle_request(
            request(utterance="Please tell me the next clue.", request_id="trace")
        )
        trace = controller.export_trace("contract-session")
        rows = trace["rows"]
        self.assertEqual(trace["session_id"], "contract-session")
        self.assertEqual(trace["session_epoch"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(trace["cursor"]["session_id"], "contract-session")
        self.assertEqual(trace["cursor"]["row_count"], len(rows))
        self.assertEqual(trace["cursor"]["head_hash"], rows[-1]["hash"])
        encoded = str(trace)
        self.assertNotIn("Please tell me the next clue.", encoded)
        self.assertNotIn("tide_marks_hint", encoded)
        self.assertTrue(all(len(row["hash"]) == 64 for row in rows))

        record = request(request_id="replay")
        with self.assertRaises(ProtocolError) as raised:
            ReplayValidator.validate([record, record], expected_session_id="contract-session")
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "replay_order_violation")
        tampered = dict(record, content_sha256="0" * 64)
        with self.assertRaises(ProtocolError) as raised:
            ReplayValidator.validate([tampered], expected_session_id="contract-session")
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "content_hash_mismatch")

    def test_p_arm_adapter_failure_is_explicit_not_rule_fallback(self) -> None:
        def failed_runner(**_kwargs):
            return 7, {"error": "timeout"}

        interpreter = IntentInterpreter(arm="P", prompt_runner=failed_runner)
        with self.assertRaises(ControllerFailure) as raised:
            interpreter.interpret(request_id="adapter-failure", utterance="Where is the lens?")
        self.assertEqual(str(raised.exception), "codex_interpretation_failed:timeout")

    def test_observation_event_accepts_null_transient_utterance(self) -> None:
        envelope = default_envelope(
            message_type="request",
            body={
                "event_kind": "observation",
                "utterance": None,
                "observation": observation(lens_reachable=True),
            },
            request_id="observation-event",
            arm="R",
            backend="rule",
        )
        validated = validate_envelope(envelope, expected_message_type="request")
        self.assertEqual(validated["body"]["event_kind"], "observation")
        self.assertIsNone(validated["body"]["utterance"])
        self.assertEqual(sha256_json({"b": 2, "a": 1}), sha256_json({"a": 1, "b": 2}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import threading
from collections.abc import Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from nesy_game.npc_goal import GoalController, Interpretation, ReplayValidator
from scripts.run_npc_service import (
    DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
    NpcServer,
    ServiceState,
)
from scripts.run_playable_evaluation import (
    SANDBOXED_MACOS_HOST_ERROR,
    _run_godot,
    find_godot_4,
    stage_project,
)

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]
GODOT_PROJECT = ROOT / "game-track" / "godot"
HARNESS = ROOT / "game-track" / "godot" / "tests" / "npc_play_loop_harness.gd"
TRANSPORT = ROOT / "game-track" / "godot" / "tests" / "npc_play_loop_transport.gd"
RESULT_PREFIX = "NPC_PLAY_LOOP_RESULT "
CAPABILITY = "npc-play-loop-test-capability"


class FakePInterpreter:
    """Explicit no-model P-arm interpreter; GoalController/planner/schema stay real."""

    arm = "P"
    model = None

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    def interpret(
        self, *, request_id: str, utterance: str, timeout_ms: int | None = None
    ) -> Interpretation:
        del timeout_ms
        self.calls.append((request_id, utterance))
        return Interpretation(
            intent_id=f"{request_id}.intent",
            typed_intent={
                "label": "ASK_INFORMATION",
                "claims": [{"code": "NONE", "state": "UNVERIFIED"}],
                "target": "signal",
            },
            backend="codex",
            arm="P",
        )


@contextmanager
def running_fake_p_service() -> Iterator[tuple[str, ServiceState, FakePInterpreter]]:
    interpreter = FakePInterpreter()
    controller = GoalController(interpreter=interpreter)
    state = ServiceState(
        controller,
        allowed_origins=DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ORIGINS,
        max_workers=1,
        trace_output=None,
        capability=CAPABILITY,
        max_inference_calls=8,
        header_timeout=0.25,
        body_timeout=0.25,
    )
    server = NpcServer("127.0.0.1", 0, state)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    try:
        yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}", state, interpreter
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()
        thread.join(timeout=2)


def _ensure_project_imported(godot: str, staged: Path) -> None:
    _run_godot(
        [
            godot,
            "--headless",
            "--path",
            str(staged),
            "--import",
            "--log-file",
            str(staged.parent / "npc-play-loop-import.log"),
        ],
        label="npc play-loop project import",
        timeout=300,
        allowed_errors=SANDBOXED_MACOS_HOST_ERROR,
    )


def _run_harness(
    godot: str,
    staged: Path,
    report_path: Path,
    *,
    real_service: bool = False,
    contract_regression: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    command = [
        godot,
        "--headless",
        "--path",
        str(staged),
        "--script",
        "res://tests/npc_play_loop_harness.gd",
        "--quit-after",
        "12000" if real_service else "1200",
        "--log-file",
        str(staged.parent / "npc-play-loop-execution.log"),
        "--",
    ]
    if real_service:
        command.extend(["--real-service", "--report-path", str(report_path)])
    else:
        command.extend(["--report-path", str(report_path)])
    if contract_regression:
        command.append("--contract-regression")
    print("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_COMMAND " + json.dumps(command))
    result = _run_godot(
        command,
        label="npc ordinary play-loop harness",
        timeout=120,
        allowed_errors=SANDBOXED_MACOS_HOST_ERROR,
    )
    output = result.stdout + result.stderr
    lines = [line for line in output.splitlines() if line.startswith(RESULT_PREFIX)]
    assert len(lines) == 1, f"play-loop harness must emit exactly one result marker:\n{output}"
    report = json.loads(lines[0][len(RESULT_PREFIX) :])
    assert report["passed"] is True, report["errors"]
    assert report["errors"] == []
    assert report_path.is_file(), report
    return report


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def godot() -> str:
    try:
        executable, _version = find_godot_4()
    except RuntimeError as error:
        pytest.skip(str(error))
    return executable


@pytest.fixture
def staged_project(godot: str, tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    staged = tmp_path / "project"
    stage_project(GODOT_PROJECT, staged)
    tests_dir = staged / "tests"
    tests_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy2(HARNESS, tests_dir / HARNESS.name)
    shutil.copy2(TRANSPORT, tests_dir / TRANSPORT.name)
    _ensure_project_imported(godot, staged)
    return staged


def test_real_scene_ordinary_play_loop_uses_one_interpretation_and_observation_recovery(
    godot: str,
    staged_project: Path,
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    source_project_hash = hashlib.sha256((GODOT_PROJECT / "project.godot").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    report_path = tmp_path / "npc-play-loop.result.json"
    monkeypatch.setenv("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY", CAPABILITY)
    with running_fake_p_service() as (endpoint, service_state, interpreter):
        monkeypatch.setenv("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_ENDPOINT", endpoint)
        report = _run_harness(godot, staged_project, report_path)
        assert report["transport_mode"] == "injected_fake_http"
        assert report["real_wait_timeout_ms"] == 35000
        assert interpreter.calls and len(interpreter.calls) == 1
        assert (
            interpreter.calls[0][1]
            == "Please help me restore the harbor signal and then reveal the tide marks hint."
        )
        expected_raw_utterance_sha = hashlib.sha256(
            interpreter.calls[0][1].encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        assert report["request_events"][0]["raw_utterance_sha256"] == expected_raw_utterance_sha
        assert (
            report["decision_provenance"][0]["raw_utterance_sha256"] == expected_raw_utterance_sha
        )
        assert (
            report["request_events"][0]["request_id"]
            == report["decision_provenance"][0]["request_id"]
        )
        assert report["events"]["event_kinds"] == [
            "utterance",
            "observation",
            "observation",
            "observation",
        ]
        assert report["events"]["request_count"] == 4
        assert report["events"]["decision_count"] == 4
        assert report["events"]["result_ack_count"] >= 1
        assert report["final_observation"]["goal_reached"] is True
        assert report["final_state_ref"]["revision"] == 3
        assert report["report_path_arg"] == "--report-path <path>"
        assert report["command_contract"].endswith("--quit-after 1200 -- --report-path <path>")
        assert report["session_id"]

        epochs = sorted(set(report["events"]["session_epochs"]))
        assert len(epochs) == 3, report["events"]
        replay_reports = []
        for epoch in epochs:
            snapshot = service_state.controller.export_trace(
                report["session_id"], session_epoch=epoch
            )
            replay = ReplayValidator.validate(snapshot, expected_session_id=report["session_id"])
            assert replay["valid"] is True
            assert replay["rows"] > 0
            replay_reports.append({"epoch": epoch, "rows": replay["rows"]})
        assert [row["epoch"] for row in replay_reports] == epochs
        assert service_state.inference_calls == 1

    assert (
        hashlib.sha256((GODOT_PROJECT / "project.godot").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        == source_project_hash
    )


def test_godot_decision_contract_rejects_invalid_status_and_target(
    godot: str,
    staged_project: Path,
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Canonical decision validation rejects invalid status, target, and terminal shape."""
    report_path = tmp_path / "npc-contract.result.json"
    monkeypatch.setenv("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY", CAPABILITY)
    with running_fake_p_service() as (endpoint, _service_state, _interpreter):
        monkeypatch.setenv("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_ENDPOINT", endpoint)
        report = _run_harness(
            godot,
            staged_project,
            report_path,
            contract_regression=True,
        )

    controls = {str(control["name"]): control for control in report["contract_controls"]}
    expected_negative = {
        "status_cancelled_rejected",
        "status_failed_rejected",
        "status_unknown_rejected",
        "arbitrary_target_rejected",
        "empty_error_rejected",
    }
    assert expected_negative <= controls.keys()
    assert all(controls[name]["passed"] is True for name in expected_negative), controls
    assert all(controls[name]["accepted"] is False for name in expected_negative), controls
    assert all(controls[name]["directive_delta"] == 0 for name in expected_negative), controls
    assert all(controls[name]["state_unchanged"] is True for name in expected_negative), controls
    assert all(
        "state_ref_before" in controls[name] and "state_ref_after" in controls[name]
        for name in expected_negative
    ), controls
    assert controls["valid_player_action"]["passed"] is True, controls
    assert controls["valid_dialogue_action"]["passed"] is True, controls
    assert "goal" in report["phases"], report


@pytest.mark.skipif(
    os.environ.get("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_REAL_SERVICE") != "1",
    reason="optional paid real-service mode; set NPC_PLAY_LOOP_REAL_SERVICE=1 explicitly",
)
def test_optional_real_service_mode_uses_existing_endpoint_and_report_contract(
    godot: str,
    staged_project: Path,
    tmp_path: Path,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """Opt-in only: parent supplies the existing endpoint and capability, never this test."""
    capability = os.environ.get("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_CAPABILITY", "").strip()
    if not capability:
        pytest.fail("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_CAPABILITY must be supplied for optional real-service mode")
    report_path = tmp_path / "npc-play-loop.real-service.result.json"
    monkeypatch.delenv("NPC_PLAY_LOOP_ENDPOINT", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.setenv("TRACE_RPG_NPC_CAPABILITY", capability)
    report = _run_harness(godot, staged_project, report_path, real_service=True)

    assert report["phases"][-1] == "goal"
    assert report["report_path_arg"] == "--report-path <path>"
    assert report["command_contract"].endswith(
        "--quit-after 12000 -- --real-service --report-path <path>"
    )
    assert report["final_observation"]["goal_reached"] is True
    assert report["errors"] == []

===== game-track/godot/tests/npc_play_loop_harness.gd | sha256:bf130a8a576037f7ad524a573852a6e8a298c4ae1e8d214003577374f990904b | bytes:24272 =====
extends SceneTree

const MAIN_SCENE := "res://scenes/main_3d.tscn"
const TRANSPORT_SCRIPT := preload("res://tests/npc_play_loop_transport.gd")
const RESULT_PREFIX := "NPC_PLAY_LOOP_RESULT "
const REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS := 35000

var failures: Array[String] = []
var errors: Array[String] = []
var frames: int = 0
var decision_count: int = 0
var ack_count: int = 0
var directive_codes: Array[String] = []
var request_events: Array = []
var decision_provenance: Array = []
var completed_phases: Array[String] = []
var report_path := ""
var real_service := false
var contract_regression := false
var contract_controls: Array = []

func _init() -> void:
	var args := OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
	real_service = "--real-service" in args
	var report_index := args.find("--report-path")
	if report_index >= 0 and report_index + 1 < args.size():
		report_path = str(args[report_index + 1])
	contract_regression = "--contract-regression" in args
	call_deferred("_run")

func _run() -> void:
	var game: Node = load(MAIN_SCENE).instantiate()
	root.add_child(game)
	await _tick(3)
	var client: NpcAgentClient = await _replace_client(game)
	client.connect("request_sent", Callable(self, "_on_request_sent"))
	_connect_observers(client, game)

	var initial_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	if real_service and str((client as Node).get("transport_mode")) != "production_super_send":
		_abort_run("real_transport_override", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	game._open_mira_dialogue()
	await _tick(1)
	_check(request_events.is_empty(), "initial_open_must_not_request_without_prior_intent")
	_check(not bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false)), "initial_goal_must_be_false")
	completed_phases.append("initial_open")

	game._on_npc_text_submitted("Please help me restore the harbor signal and then reveal the tide marks hint.")
	var utterance_request_ready := await _wait_for_requests_deadline(request_events, 1, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS) if real_service else await _wait_for_requests(request_events, 1)
	_check(utterance_request_ready, "utterance_request_must_complete")
	if not utterance_request_ready:
		_abort_run("utterance_request", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	var acquire_directive_ready := await _wait_for_waiting_action(game, "acquire_object")
	_check(acquire_directive_ready, "utterance_must_issue_acquire_directive")
	if not acquire_directive_ready:
		_abort_run("acquire_directive", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	_check(request_events.size() >= 1 and str(request_events[0].get("event_kind", "")) == "utterance", "first_request_must_be_utterance")
	completed_phases.append("utterance")

	game._on_choice("leave")
	await _tick(1)
	var after_close_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(after_close_ref == initial_ref, "ordinary_dialogue_close_must_not_mutate_world")
	completed_phases.append("close")
	game._propose_acquire()
	await _tick(1)
	var acquired_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(int(acquired_ref["revision"]) == int(initial_ref["revision"]) + 1, "explicit_acquire_must_increment_revision_once")
	_check(str(acquired_ref["sha256"]) != str(initial_ref["sha256"]), "explicit_acquire_must_change_state_hash")
	completed_phases.append("acquire")

	game._open_mira_dialogue()
	var install_request_ready := await _wait_for_requests(request_events, 2)
	_check(install_request_ready, "first_resume_observation_must_complete")
	if not install_request_ready:
		_abort_run("install_observation", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	var install_directive_ready := await _wait_for_waiting_action(game, "install_lens")
	_check(install_directive_ready, "first_resume_must_issue_install_directive")
	if not install_directive_ready:
		_abort_run("install_directive", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	_check(str(request_events[1].get("event_kind", "")) == "observation", "first_resume_must_be_observation_only")
	completed_phases.append("resume_install")
	game._on_choice("leave")
	await _tick(1)
	completed_phases.append("close")
	game._propose_install()
	await _tick(1)
	var installed_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(int(installed_ref["revision"]) == int(acquired_ref["revision"]) + 1, "explicit_install_must_increment_revision_once")
	_check(str(installed_ref["sha256"]) != str(acquired_ref["sha256"]), "explicit_install_must_change_state_hash")
	_check(bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("signal_restored", false)), "explicit_install_must_restore_signal")
	_check(bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("tide_lead_authorized", false)), "install_must_authorize_mira_lead")
	completed_phases.append("install")

	game._open_mira_dialogue()
	var reveal_requests_ready := await _wait_for_requests(request_events, 4)
	_check(reveal_requests_ready, "second_resume_and_result_observations_must_complete")
	if not reveal_requests_ready:
		_abort_run("reveal_observations", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	var goal_ready := await _wait_for_goal(game)
	_check(goal_ready, "authorized_reveal_must_reach_goal")
	if not goal_ready:
		_abort_run("goal_reached", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	var ack_ready := await _wait_for_acks(ack_count, 1)
	_check(ack_ready, "reveal_result_ack_must_be_received")
	if not ack_ready:
		_abort_run("result_ack", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	var terminal_decision_ready := await _wait_for_decisions(4)
	_check(terminal_decision_ready, "post_ack_observation_decision_must_complete")
	if not terminal_decision_ready:
		_abort_run("terminal_observation", game, client, initial_ref)
		return
	_check(str(request_events[2].get("event_kind", "")) == "observation", "second_resume_must_be_observation_only")
	_check(str(request_events[3].get("event_kind", "")) == "observation", "post_ack_replan_must_be_observation_only")
	completed_phases.append("resume_reveal")
	var final_observation: Dictionary = game.npc_public_observation()
	var final_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(int(final_ref["revision"]) == int(installed_ref["revision"]) + 1, "reveal_must_increment_revision_once")
	_check(str(final_ref["sha256"]) != str(installed_ref["sha256"]), "reveal_must_change_state_hash")
	_check(bool(final_observation.get("goal_reached", false)), "final_observation_must_report_goal_reached")
	_check("tide_marks_hint" in game.machine.state["facts"], "authorized_hint_must_be_committed_to_world")
	completed_phases.append("goal")

	if contract_regression:
		await _run_contract_controls()
	await _run_stale_and_cancel_controls()
	var frame_limit := "12000" if real_service else "1200"
	var command_contract := "godot --headless --path <staged_project> --script res://tests/npc_play_loop_harness.gd --quit-after %s -- %s--report-path <path>" % [frame_limit, "--real-service " if real_service else ""]
	var report := {
		"mode": "real_service" if real_service else "fake_p_interpreter",
		"transport_mode": str((client as Node).get("transport_mode")),
		"real_wait_timeout_ms": REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS,
		"passed": failures.is_empty() and errors.is_empty(),
		"phases": completed_phases.duplicate(),
		"frames": frames,
		"report_path": report_path,
		"report_path_arg": "--report-path <path>",
		"command_contract": command_contract,
		"events": {
			"request_count": request_events.size(),
			"event_kinds": _request_kinds(),
			"session_epochs": _request_epochs(),
			"decision_count": decision_count,
			"result_ack_count": ack_count,
			"directive_codes": directive_codes.duplicate(),
		},
		"decision_provenance": decision_provenance.duplicate(true),
		"request_events": request_events.duplicate(true),
		"initial_state_ref": initial_ref,
		"installed_state_ref": installed_ref,
		"final_state_ref": final_ref,
		"final_observation": final_observation,
		"session_id": client.launch_identity().get("session_id", "") if client != null else "",
		"contract_controls": contract_controls.duplicate(true),
		"errors": failures + errors,
	}
	_write_report(report)
	quit(0 if bool(report["passed"]) else 1)

func _replace_client(game: Node) -> NpcAgentClient:
	var old_client: Node = game.npc_client
	old_client.queue_free()
	await process_frame
	var replacement: NpcAgentClient = TRANSPORT_SCRIPT.new()
	game.add_child(replacement)
	await process_frame
	game.npc_client = replacement
	replacement.setup(game, game.ui)
	return replacement

func _connect_observers(client: NpcAgentClient, game: Node) -> void:
	client.decision_received.connect(Callable(game, "_on_npc_decision"))
	client.result_recorded.connect(Callable(game, "_on_npc_result_recorded"))
	client.directive_received.connect(Callable(game, "_on_npc_directive"))
	client.protocol_error.connect(Callable(game, "_on_npc_protocol_error"))
	client.decision_received.connect(Callable(self, "_on_decision"))
	client.result_recorded.connect(Callable(self, "_on_ack"))
	client.directive_received.connect(Callable(self, "_on_directive"))
	client.protocol_error.connect(Callable(self, "_on_protocol_error"))

func _on_request_sent(path: String, envelope: Dictionary) -> void:
	if path != "/npc/step":
		return
	request_events.append({
		"path": path,
		"event_kind": str(envelope.get("body", {}).get("event_kind", "")),
		"session_epoch": int(envelope.get("session_epoch", -1)),
		"request_id": str(envelope.get("request_id", "")),
		"raw_utterance_sha256": str(envelope.get("provenance", {}).get("raw_utterance_sha256", "")),
	})

func _on_decision(envelope: Dictionary, context: Dictionary) -> void:
	decision_count += 1
	decision_provenance.append({
		"event_kind": str(context.get("event_kind", "")),
		"session_epoch": int(context.get("session_epoch", -1)),
		"request_id": str(envelope.get("request_id", "")),
		"raw_utterance_sha256": str(envelope.get("provenance", {}).get("raw_utterance_sha256", "")),
	})
func _on_ack(_envelope: Dictionary, _context: Dictionary) -> void:
	ack_count += 1

func _on_directive(code: String, _payload: Dictionary) -> void:
	directive_codes.append(code)

func _on_protocol_error(reason: String) -> void:
	errors.append(reason)

func _abort_run(phase: String, game: Node, client: NpcAgentClient, initial_ref: Dictionary) -> void:
	failures.append("HARNESS_ABORTED_%s" % phase)
	var frame_limit := "12000" if real_service else "1200"
	var command_contract := "godot --headless --path <staged_project> --script res://tests/npc_play_loop_harness.gd --quit-after %s -- %s--report-path <path>" % [frame_limit, "--real-service " if real_service else ""]
	var report := {
		"mode": "real_service" if real_service else "fake_p_interpreter",
		"transport_mode": str((client as Node).get("transport_mode")),
		"real_wait_timeout_ms": REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS,
		"passed": false,
		"fatal_phase": phase,
		"phases": completed_phases.duplicate(),
		"frames": frames,
		"report_path": report_path,
		"report_path_arg": "--report-path <path>",
		"command_contract": command_contract,
		"events": {
			"request_count": request_events.size(),
			"event_kinds": _request_kinds(),
			"session_epochs": _request_epochs(),
			"decision_count": decision_count,
			"result_ack_count": ack_count,
			"directive_codes": directive_codes.duplicate(),
		},
		"decision_provenance": decision_provenance.duplicate(true),
		"request_events": request_events.duplicate(true),
		"initial_state_ref": initial_ref,
		"final_state_ref": game.npc_state_ref(),
		"final_observation": game.npc_public_observation(),
		"session_id": client.launch_identity().get("session_id", ""),
		"errors": failures + errors,
	}
	_write_report(report)
	quit(1)

func _wait_for_requests_deadline(events: Array, expected: int, timeout_ms: int) -> bool:
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + timeout_ms
	while events.size() < expected and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return events.size() >= expected

func _wait_for_waiting_action_deadline(game: Node, operation: String, timeout_ms: int) -> bool:
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + timeout_ms
	while str(game._npc_waiting_action.get("operation", "")) != operation and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return str(game._npc_waiting_action.get("operation", "")) == operation

func _wait_for_goal_deadline(game: Node, timeout_ms: int) -> bool:
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + timeout_ms
	while not bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false)) and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false))

func _wait_for_acks_deadline(current: int, expected: int, timeout_ms: int) -> bool:
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + timeout_ms
	while ack_count < expected and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return ack_count >= expected and ack_count >= current

func _wait_for_decisions_deadline(expected: int, timeout_ms: int) -> bool:
	var deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + timeout_ms
	while decision_count < expected and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return decision_count >= expected

func _wait_for_requests(events: Array, expected: int, max_frames: int = 600) -> bool:
	if real_service:
		return await _wait_for_requests_deadline(events, expected, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS)
	for _index in range(max_frames):
		if events.size() >= expected:
			return true
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return events.size() >= expected

func _wait_for_waiting_action(game: Node, operation: String, max_frames: int = 600) -> bool:
	if real_service:
		return await _wait_for_waiting_action_deadline(game, operation, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS)
	for _index in range(max_frames):
		if str(game._npc_waiting_action.get("operation", "")) == operation:
			return true
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return str(game._npc_waiting_action.get("operation", "")) == operation

func _wait_for_goal(game: Node, max_frames: int = 600) -> bool:
	if real_service:
		return await _wait_for_goal_deadline(game, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS)
	for _index in range(max_frames):
		if bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false)):
			return true
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return bool(game.npc_public_observation().get("goal_reached", false))

func _wait_for_acks(current: int, expected: int, max_frames: int = 600) -> bool:
	if real_service:
		return await _wait_for_acks_deadline(current, expected, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS)
	for _index in range(max_frames):
		if ack_count >= expected:
			return true
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return ack_count >= expected and ack_count >= current

func _wait_for_decisions(expected: int, max_frames: int = 600) -> bool:
	if real_service:
		return await _wait_for_decisions_deadline(expected, REAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT_MS)
	for _index in range(max_frames):
		if decision_count >= expected:
			return true
		await process_frame
		frames += 1
	return decision_count >= expected

func _run_contract_controls() -> void:
	var control_game: Node = load(MAIN_SCENE).instantiate()
	root.add_child(control_game)
	await _tick(2)
	var control_client: NpcAgentClient = await _replace_client(control_game)
	_connect_observers(control_client, control_game)

	var player_steps := [{
		"action_id": "contract-valid-player",
		"operation": "acquire_object",
		"execution_mode": "player_request",
		"arguments": {"object_id": "signal_lens"},
	}]
	var player_before := directive_codes.size()
	var player_result := _contract_decision(control_client, control_game, _contract_body("WAITING_PLAYER", "signal", player_steps))
	var player_directive_delta := directive_codes.size() - player_before
	var player_passed := bool(player_result.get("accepted", false)) and player_directive_delta == 1
	contract_controls.append({"name": "valid_player_action", "accepted": bool(player_result.get("accepted", false)), "directive_delta": player_directive_delta, "passed": player_passed})
	_check(player_passed, "valid_player_action_control_must_be_accepted")

	for status in ["CANCELLED", "FAILED", "UNKNOWN"]:
		var action_id := "contract-%s" % str(status).to_lower()
		var steps := [{
			"action_id": action_id,
			"operation": "acquire_object",
			"execution_mode": "player_request",
			"arguments": {"object_id": "signal_lens"},
		}]
		var before_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
		var before := directive_codes.size()
		var result := _contract_decision(control_client, control_game, _contract_body(status, "signal", steps))
		var after_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
		var delta := directive_codes.size() - before
		var state_unchanged := before_ref == after_ref
		var passed := not bool(result.get("accepted", false)) and delta == 0 and state_unchanged
		contract_controls.append({"name": "status_%s_rejected" % str(status).to_lower(), "accepted": bool(result.get("accepted", false)), "directive_delta": delta, "state_unchanged": state_unchanged, "state_ref_before": before_ref.duplicate(true), "state_ref_after": after_ref.duplicate(true), "codes": result.get("codes", []), "passed": passed})
		_check(passed, "status_%s_must_not_issue_directive" % str(status).to_lower())

	var arbitrary_target_steps := [{
		"action_id": "contract-arbitrary-target",
		"operation": "acquire_object",
		"execution_mode": "player_request",
		"arguments": {"object_id": "signal_lens"},
	}]
	var arbitrary_before_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
	var arbitrary_before := directive_codes.size()
	var arbitrary_result := _contract_decision(control_client, control_game, _contract_body("WAITING_PLAYER", "arbitrary_target", arbitrary_target_steps))
	var arbitrary_after_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
	var arbitrary_delta := directive_codes.size() - arbitrary_before
	var arbitrary_state_unchanged := arbitrary_before_ref == arbitrary_after_ref
	var arbitrary_passed := not bool(arbitrary_result.get("accepted", false)) and arbitrary_delta == 0 and arbitrary_state_unchanged
	contract_controls.append({"name": "arbitrary_target_rejected", "accepted": bool(arbitrary_result.get("accepted", false)), "directive_delta": arbitrary_delta, "state_unchanged": arbitrary_state_unchanged, "state_ref_before": arbitrary_before_ref.duplicate(true), "state_ref_after": arbitrary_after_ref.duplicate(true), "codes": arbitrary_result.get("codes", []), "passed": arbitrary_passed})
	_check(arbitrary_passed, "arbitrary_target_must_not_issue_directive")

	var empty_before_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
	var empty_before := directive_codes.size()
	var empty_result := _contract_decision(control_client, control_game, _contract_body("ERROR", null, []))
	var empty_after_ref: Dictionary = control_game.npc_state_ref()
	var empty_delta := directive_codes.size() - empty_before
	var empty_state_unchanged := empty_before_ref == empty_after_ref
	var empty_passed := not bool(empty_result.get("accepted", false)) and empty_delta == 0 and empty_state_unchanged
	contract_controls.append({"name": "empty_error_rejected", "accepted": bool(empty_result.get("accepted", false)), "directive_delta": empty_delta, "state_unchanged": empty_state_unchanged, "state_ref_before": empty_before_ref.duplicate(true), "state_ref_after": empty_after_ref.duplicate(true), "codes": empty_result.get("codes", []), "passed": empty_passed})
	_check(empty_passed, "empty_error_must_not_be_accepted")

	control_game._on_npc_cancel_requested()
	await _tick(1)
	control_game._open_mira_dialogue()
	await _tick(1)
	var dialogue_steps := [{
		"action_id": "contract-valid-dialogue",
		"operation": "clarify",
		"execution_mode": "dialogue",
		"arguments": {},
	}]
	var dialogue_before := directive_codes.size()
	var dialogue_result := _contract_decision(control_client, control_game, _contract_body("WAITING_DIALOGUE", null, dialogue_steps))
	var dialogue_delta := directive_codes.size() - dialogue_before
	var dialogue_passed := bool(dialogue_result.get("accepted", false)) and dialogue_delta == 1
	contract_controls.append({"name": "valid_dialogue_action", "accepted": bool(dialogue_result.get("accepted", false)), "directive_delta": dialogue_delta, "passed": dialogue_passed})
	_check(dialogue_passed, "valid_dialogue_control_must_be_accepted")
	control_game.queue_free()

func _contract_body(status: String, target: Variant, steps: Array) -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"intent_id": "contract-%s" % status.to_lower(),
		"typed_intent": {"label": "ASK_INFORMATION", "claims": [{"code": "NONE", "state": "UNVERIFIED"}], "target": target},
		"plan_id": "contract-plan",
		"steps": steps,
		"planning_context_digest": "a".repeat(64),
		"status": status,
		"error": null,
	}

func _contract_context(client: NpcAgentClient, state_ref: Dictionary, request_id: String) -> Dictionary:
	var identity := client.launch_identity()
	return {
		"generation": int(client.get("_request_generation")),
		"session_epoch": int(identity.get("session_epoch", -1)),
		"session_id": str(identity.get("session_id", "")),
		"episode_id": str(identity.get("episode_id", "")),
		"request_id": request_id,
		"event_kind": "observation",
		"goal_id": NpcAgentClient.GOAL_ID,
		"goal_version": NpcAgentClient.GOAL_VERSION,
		"policy_hash": str(identity.get("policy_hash", "")),
		"config_hash": str(identity.get("config_hash", "")),
		"expected_revision": int(state_ref.get("revision", -1)),
		"expected_sha256": str(state_ref.get("sha256", "")),
		"deadline_ms": Time.get_ticks_msec() + 30000,
	}

func _contract_decision(client: NpcAgentClient, game: Node, body: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
	var state_ref: Dictionary = game.npc_state_ref()
	var request_id := "request-%s" % str(body.get("intent_id", ""))
	var envelope := client._build_envelope("decision", request_id, body, state_ref, "")
	var context := _contract_context(client, state_ref, request_id)
	return client.accept_decision(envelope, context, state_ref)

func _run_stale_and_cancel_controls() -> void:
	var stale_game: Node = load(MAIN_SCENE).instantiate()
	root.add_child(stale_game)
	await _tick(2)
	var stale_initial: Dictionary = stale_game.npc_state_ref()
	stale_game._npc_waiting_action = {
		"operation": "acquire_object",
		"action_id": "stale-play-loop",
		"context": {"expected_revision": int(stale_initial.get("revision", -1))},
	}
	var mutation: Dictionary = stale_game.machine.apply_operation("acquire_object", {"object_id": "signal_lens"})
	var stale_before: Dictionary = stale_game.npc_state_ref()
	stale_game._propose_acquire()
	var stale_after: Dictionary = stale_game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(bool(mutation.get("accepted", false)), "stale-control-setup-mutation_must_apply")
	_check(stale_before == stale_after and stale_game._npc_waiting_action.is_empty(), "stale_directive_must_not_reach_writer")
	stale_game.queue_free()

	var cancel_game: Node = load(MAIN_SCENE).instantiate()
	root.add_child(cancel_game)
	await _tick(2)
	var cancel_initial: Dictionary = cancel_game.npc_state_ref()
	cancel_game._npc_waiting_action = {
		"operation": "acquire_object",
		"action_id": "cancelled-play-loop",
		"context": {"expected_revision": int(cancel_initial.get("revision", -1))},
	}
	cancel_game._on_npc_cancel_requested()
	var ordinary_before: Dictionary = cancel_game.npc_state_ref()
	cancel_game._propose_acquire()
	var ordinary_after: Dictionary = cancel_game.npc_state_ref()
	_check(int(ordinary_after["revision"]) == int(ordinary_before["revision"]) + 1 and bool(cancel_game.npc_public_observation().get("lens_carried", false)), "ordinary_player_action_must_work_after_cancel")
	cancel_game.queue_free()

func _request_kinds() -> Array:
	var values: Array = []
	for event in request_events:
		values.append(str(event.get("event_kind", "")))
	return values

func _request_epochs() -> Array:
	var values: Array = []
	for event in request_events:
		values.append(int(event.get("session_epoch", -1)))
	return values

func _tick(count: int) -> void:
	for _index in range(count):
		await process_frame
		frames += 1

func _check(condition: bool, message: String) -> void:
	if not condition:
		failures.append(message)

func _write_report(report: Dictionary) -> void:
	var serialized := JSON.stringify(report, "  ")
	if not report_path.is_empty():
		var absolute := ProjectSettings.globalize_path(report_path)
		var file := FileAccess.open(absolute, FileAccess.WRITE)
		if file == null:
			report["passed"] = false
			report["errors"].append("REPORT_WRITE_FAILED")
		else:
			file.store_string(serialized)
			file.close()
	print(RESULT_PREFIX + JSON.stringify(report))
