===== tests/test_generate_npc_paper_results_model_cohort.py | sha256:9385bdc5c0f87aa5dc0f361b7fc63899d02c4caeb6de4ee08a6dd32c9b3498f2 | bytes:15637 =====
import json
import os
import sys
import unittest
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
from unittest.mock import patch

ROOT = Path(__file__).parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "scripts"))

import generate_npc_paper_results as generator


class GenerateNpcPaperResultsModelCohortTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def _load_packet_with_model_mutation(self, mutate):
        original_rows = generator._rows

        def rows(path):
            records = original_rows(path)
            if path.name == "screening-results.jsonl":
                p_rows = [row for row in records if row.get("arm") == "P"]
                mutate(p_rows)
            return records

        with patch.object(generator, "_rows", side_effect=rows):
            return generator._load_packet()

    def _load_packet_with_screening_mutation(self, mutate):
        original_rows = generator._rows

        def rows(path):
            records = original_rows(path)
            if path.name == "screening-results.jsonl":
                mutate(records)
            return records

        with patch.object(generator, "_rows", side_effect=rows):
            return generator._load_packet()

    @staticmethod
    def _set_model_cohort(rows, requested, returned):
        for row in rows:
            row["model"].update(requested=requested, returned=returned)

    def test_uniform_null_cohort_is_accepted_and_disclosed_in_both_languages(self):
        packet = self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(
            lambda rows: self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested=None, returned=None)
        )
        metadata = packet["model_metadata"]

        self.assertIsNone(metadata["requested"])
        self.assertIsNone(metadata["returned"])
        self.assertEqual(metadata["returned_unknown_count"], metadata["cohort_size"])
        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)

        self.assertIn("Requested model: unknown (null)", english)
        self.assertIn("recorded decision-model identifier: unknown (null)", english)
        self.assertIn("Actual model-family matching is unverified", english)
        self.assertIn("요청 모델: 알 수 없음 (null)", korean)
        self.assertIn("decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: 알 수 없음 (null)", korean)
        self.assertIn("실제 모델 계열 일치는 unverified(검증되지 않음)", korean)

    def test_returned_named_and_null_rows_are_disclosed_as_partial_unknown(self):
        def mutate(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested=None, returned=None)
            rows[0]["model"].update(returned="rev-test")

        packet = self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)
        metadata = packet["model_metadata"]
        unknown_count = metadata["cohort_size"] - 1
        self.assertEqual(metadata["returned"], "rev-test")
        self.assertEqual(metadata["returned_unknown_count"], unknown_count)

        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)
        self.assertIn(
            f"recorded decision-model identifier: rev-test (partial; unknown {unknown_count}/{metadata['cohort_size']})",
            english,
        )
        self.assertIn(
            f"decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: rev-test (부분; 알 수 없음 {unknown_count}/{metadata['cohort_size']})",
            korean,
        )

    def test_mixed_or_differing_requested_cohorts_are_rejected(self):
        def mixed_requested(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested=None, returned=None)
            rows[0]["model"].update(requested="codex-test")

        def differing_requested(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested=None, returned=None)
            rows[0]["model"].update(requested="codex-a")
            rows[1]["model"].update(requested="codex-b")

        cases = {
            "mixed requested null and named": mixed_requested,
            "differing requested names": differing_requested,
        }
        for name, mutate in cases.items():
            with (
                self.subTest(name=name),
                self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, r"requested model cohort is mixed"),
            ):
                self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)

    def test_differing_returned_model_names_are_rejected(self):
        def mutate(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested=None, returned=None)
            rows[0]["model"].update(returned="rev-a")
            rows[1]["model"].update(returned="rev-b")

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(
            ValueError, r"returned model cohort has multiple named revisions"
        ):
            self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)

    def test_missing_or_malformed_model_metadata_is_rejected(self):
        cases = {
            "missing model object": lambda rows: rows[0].pop("model"),
            "missing requested field": lambda rows: rows[0]["model"].pop("requested"),
            "missing returned field": lambda rows: rows[0]["model"].pop("returned"),
            "model object is not mapping": lambda rows: rows[0].update(model="codex-test"),
            "requested name has surrounding whitespace": lambda rows: rows[0]["model"].update(
                requested=" codex-test "
            ),
            "returned metadata has wrong type": lambda rows: rows[0]["model"].update(returned=7),
        }
        for name, mutate in cases.items():
            with (
                self.subTest(name=name),
                self.assertRaisesRegex(
                    ValueError, r"model metadata|model (?:requested|returned) malformed"
                ),
            ):
                self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)

    def test_explicit_model_names_are_latex_escaped_in_bilingual_results(self):
        def mutate(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested="Model_v1%safe&#", returned="rev_1")

        packet = self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)
        self.assertEqual(packet["model_metadata"]["requested"], "Model_v1%safe&#")
        self.assertEqual(packet["model_metadata"]["returned"], "rev_1")

        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)
        escaped_name = r"Model\_v1\%safe\&\#"

        self.assertIn(escaped_name, english)
        self.assertIn(escaped_name, korean)
        self.assertIn(r"recorded decision-model identifier: rev\_1", english)
        self.assertIn(r"decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: rev\_1", korean)
        self.assertNotIn("provider revision: rev_1", english)
        self.assertNotIn("provider revision: rev_1", korean)
        self.assertNotIn("Model_v1%safe&#", english)
        self.assertNotIn("Model_v1%safe&#", korean)

    def test_explicit_requested_model_name_renders_without_attributing_null_return(self):
        def mutate(rows):
            self._set_model_cohort(rows, requested="gpt-5.6-sol", returned=None)

        packet = self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(mutate)
        self.assertEqual(packet["model_metadata"]["requested"], "gpt-5.6-sol")
        self.assertIsNone(packet["model_metadata"]["returned"])

        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)
        self.assertIn("Requested model: gpt-5.6-sol", english)
        self.assertIn("recorded decision-model identifier: unknown (null)", english)
        self.assertIn("요청 모델: gpt-5.6-sol", korean)
        self.assertIn("decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: 알 수 없음 (null)", korean)

    def test_recorded_selector_is_not_provider_revision_and_null_revision_is_disclosed(self):
        packet = self._load_packet_with_model_mutation(
            lambda rows: self._set_model_cohort(
                rows, requested="gpt-5.6-sol", returned="gpt-5.6-sol"
            )
        )
        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)

        self.assertIn("Requested model: gpt-5.6-sol", english)
        self.assertIn("recorded decision-model identifier: gpt-5.6-sol", english)
        self.assertIn("provider revision is unavailable", english)
        self.assertNotIn("provider revision: gpt-5.6-sol", english)
        self.assertIn("요청 모델: gpt-5.6-sol", korean)
        self.assertIn("decision에 기록된 모델 식별자: gpt-5.6-sol", korean)
        self.assertIn("provider revision은 사용할 수 없고 null 식별자는 미상으로 남긴다", korean)
        self.assertNotIn("provider revision: gpt-5.6-sol", korean)

    def test_stale_control_disclosure_retains_all_failures_and_counts_verified_controls(self):
        packet = generator._load_packet()
        disclosure = packet["stale_disclosure"]

        self.assertEqual(disclosure["assigned_failure_rows"], 12)
        self.assertEqual(disclosure["verified_rows"], 12)
        self.assertEqual(disclosure["unclassified_rows"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(disclosure["by_arm"], {"P": 4, "R": 4, "O": 4})
        self.assertEqual(disclosure["by_control"], {"stale_revision": 6, "stale_policy": 6})
        self.assertEqual(
            disclosure["by_case"],
            {"SCR-T01": ["stale_revision"], "SCR-T02": ["stale_policy"]},
        )

        english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
        korean = generator._results(packet, korean=True)
        self.assertIn(
            "Verified pre-authored stale-control ACK rejections are P=4, R=4, O=4 per arm", english
        )
        self.assertIn("SCR-T01 (stale\\_revision), SCR-T02 (stale\\_policy)", english)
        self.assertIn("12 instances had all corresponding negative-control gates passed", english)
        self.assertIn("negative-control gates", english)
        self.assertNotIn("expected_negative_gates", english)
        self.assertIn("not method-performance outcomes", english)
        self.assertIn("검증된 사전 작성 stale-control ACK 거부는 arm별 P=4, R=4, O=4건", korean)
        self.assertIn("SCR-T01 (stale\\_revision), SCR-T02 (stale\\_policy)", korean)
        self.assertIn("12개 인스턴스 모두 대응하는 음성 대조 gate가 모두 통과했다", korean)
        self.assertNotIn("expected_negative_gates", korean)
        self.assertIn("method-performance 결과로 해석하지 않으며", korean)

    def test_changed_ack_or_negative_gate_cannot_gain_controlled_failure_label(self):
        def mutate_ack(rows):
            row = next(
                row for row in rows if row.get("case_id") == "SCR-T01" and row.get("arm") == "P"
            )
            row["acks"][0]["body"]["error"] = "not_stale_result"

        def mutate_gate(rows):
            row = next(
                row for row in rows if row.get("case_id") == "SCR-T01" and row.get("arm") == "P"
            )
            row["engine"]["experiment"]["trace"]["expected_negative_gates"][0]["passed"] = False

        for name, mutate in (("changed ACK", mutate_ack), ("failed negative gate", mutate_gate)):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                packet = self._load_packet_with_screening_mutation(mutate)
                disclosure = packet["stale_disclosure"]
                self.assertEqual(disclosure["assigned_failure_rows"], 12)
                self.assertEqual(disclosure["verified_rows"], 11)
                self.assertEqual(disclosure["unclassified_rows"], 1)
                self.assertEqual(disclosure["by_arm"], {"P": 3, "R": 4, "O": 4})
                english = generator._results(packet, korean=False)
                self.assertIn(
                    "11 instances had all corresponding negative-control gates passed", english
                )
                self.assertIn("other assigned failures (1) remain unclassified", english)
                self.assertNotIn("expected_negative_gates", english)

    def test_generated_validation_reports_use_project_relative_packet_dir_and_preserve_outcomes(
        self,
    ):
        expected_packet_dir = "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"
        expected_outcomes = {
            "NPC-ENG1": {"valid": True, "publishable": False, "errors": []},
            "NPC-SCR1": {"valid": True, "publishable": True, "errors": []},
        }

        with TemporaryDirectory(dir=ROOT) as temporary_directory:
            output_root = Path(temporary_directory)
            output_dir = output_root / "generated"
            figures_dir = output_root / "figures"
            original_cwd = Path.cwd()
            os.chdir(output_root)
            try:
                with (
                    patch.object(generator, "OUT", output_dir),
                    patch.object(generator, "FIGURES", figures_dir),
                ):
                    generator.main()
            finally:
                os.chdir(original_cwd)

            reports = json.loads(
                (output_dir / "npc_validation_reports.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )

        self.assertEqual(set(reports), set(expected_outcomes))
        for packet_id, outcome in expected_outcomes.items():
            with self.subTest(packet_id=packet_id):
                report = reports[packet_id]
                self.assertEqual(report["packet_dir"], expected_packet_dir)
                self.assertFalse(Path(report["packet_dir"]).is_absolute())
                self.assertEqual(
                    {key: report[key] for key in ("valid", "publishable", "errors")},
                    outcome,
                )

    def test_portable_report_only_normalizes_exact_packet_and_preserves_payload(self):
        expected_packet_dir = "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"
        source_report = generator._load_packet()["validation_reports"]["NPC-SCR1"]
        same_basename_elsewhere = ROOT / "tests" / "not-the-npc-packet" / generator.PACKET.name
        cases = {
            "canonical packet": (str(generator.PACKET), expected_packet_dir),
            "same basename elsewhere": (str(same_basename_elsewhere), str(same_basename_elsewhere)),
            "missing packet_dir": (None, None),
            "non-string packet_dir": (7, 7),
        }

        for name, (packet_dir, expected) in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                report = deepcopy(source_report)
                if name == "missing packet_dir":
                    report.pop("packet_dir")
                else:
                    report["packet_dir"] = packet_dir
                before = deepcopy(report)

                portable = generator._portable_validation_report(report)

                self.assertEqual(report, before)
                self.assertEqual(portable["valid"], True)
                self.assertEqual(portable["publishable"], True)
                self.assertEqual(portable["errors"], [])
                if name == "missing packet_dir":
                    self.assertNotIn("packet_dir", portable)
                else:
                    self.assertEqual(portable["packet_dir"], expected)
                if name == "canonical packet":
                    self.assertFalse(Path(portable["packet_dir"]).is_absolute())
                elif name == "same basename elsewhere":
                    self.assertTrue(Path(portable["packet_dir"]).is_absolute())
                self.assertEqual(portable["source"], before["source"])
                self.assertEqual(portable["counts"], before["counts"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
POLICY_HASH = "2e7966bf2ac6b54bfaf2db84b3f446686436bd3a1f8efea592ffa82e91249edd"


class NpcEvidenceFixtureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def _load(self, name: str) -> dict:
        path = P / "data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery" / name
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_frozen_packets_have_exact_assignments_and_regime_coverage(self) -> None:
        engineering = self._load("engineering.json")
        screening = self._load("screening.json")
        self.assertEqual(engineering["schema_version"], "npc-goal-recovery/1.0.0")
        self.assertEqual(screening["schema_version"], "npc-goal-recovery/1.0.0")
        self.assertEqual(engineering["packet_id"], "NPC-ENG1")
        self.assertEqual(screening["packet_id"], "NPC-SCR1")
        self.assertEqual(len(engineering["cases"]), 16)
        self.assertEqual(len(screening["cases"]), 12)
        self.assertEqual(
            Counter(row["regime"] for row in screening["cases"]),
            Counter(
                {
                    "static": 2,
                    "language_claim": 2,
                    "world_change": 2,
                    "stale": 2,
                    "deadline_cancel": 2,
                    "duplicate_no_route": 2,
                }
            ),
        )
        self.assertEqual(engineering["provenance"]["source_kind"], "same_model_family_auxiliary")
        self.assertEqual(screening["provenance"]["source_kind"], "same_model_family_auxiliary")
        self.assertEqual(engineering["provenance"]["independent_source_fraction"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(screening["provenance"]["independent_source_fraction"], 0)

    def test_each_case_is_public_input_bound_and_hashable_before_assignment(self) -> None:
        for packet_name in ("engineering.json", "screening.json"):
            packet = self._load(packet_name)
            self.assertEqual(packet["policy_hash"], POLICY_HASH)
            ids = set()
            seeds = set()
            for case in packet["cases"]:
                with self.subTest(packet=packet_name, case=case["id"]):
                    self.assertNotIn(case["id"], ids)
                    ids.add(case["id"])
                    self.assertNotIn(case["seed"], seeds)
                    seeds.add(case["seed"])
                    self.assertIsInstance(case["utterance"], str)
                    self.assertIn("observation", case)
                    self.assertEqual(
                        set(case["observation"]),
                        {
                            "lens_reachable",
                            "lens_carried",
                            "signal_restored",
                            "tide_lead_authorized",
                            "goal_reached",
                            "in_dialogue",
                        },
                    )
                    self.assertTrue(SHA256.fullmatch(case["state_ref"]["sha256"]))
                    self.assertEqual(case["source_kind"], "same_model_family_auxiliary")
                    reference = case["reference_intent"]
                    self.assertIn(
                        reference["label"],
                        {
                            "ASK_INFORMATION",
                            "REQUEST_DISCLOSURE",
                            "REPORT_FACT",
                            "REFUSE_HELP",
                            "AMBIGUOUS",
                            "UNKNOWN",
                        },
                    )
                    for claim in reference["claims"]:
                        self.assertIn(
                            claim["code"],
                            {
                                "LENS_REACHABLE",
                                "LENS_CARRIED",
                                "SIGNAL_RESTORED",
                                "TIDE_LEAD_AUTHORIZED",
                                "GOAL_REACHED",
                                "IN_DIALOGUE",
                                "NONE",
                            },
                        )
                        self.assertEqual(claim["state"], "UNVERIFIED")

    def test_assignment_rows_do_not_persist_raw_transient_hash_as_text(self) -> None:
        for packet_name in ("engineering.json", "screening.json"):
            packet = self._load(packet_name)
            for case in packet["cases"]:
                self.assertNotIn("raw_utterance", case)
                self.assertNotIn("user_text", case.get("trace", {}))
                self.assertNotIn("private", json.dumps(case, ensure_ascii=False).lower())

    def _verified_dir(self) -> Path:
        return P / "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"

    def _copy_verified(self) -> tuple[tempfile.TemporaryDirectory[str], Path]:
        source = self._verified_dir()
        self.assertTrue(source.is_dir(), f"missing canonical final evidence directory: {source}")
        holder = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        destination = Path(holder.name) / "verified-run-004"
        shutil.copytree(source, destination)
        return holder, destination

    def _copy_portable_project(self) -> tuple[tempfile.TemporaryDirectory[str], Path, Path]:
        holder = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        root = Path(holder.name) / "relocated-project"
        root.mkdir()
        packet = root / "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"
        shutil.copytree(self._verified_dir(), packet)
        required = (
            "configs/npc-goal-recovery.json",
            "data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/engineering.json",
            "data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/screening.json",
            "game-track/schemas/npc-goal-recovery.schema.json",
            "game-track/godot/project.godot",
            "game-track/godot/data/npc-goal-recovery.json",
            "game-track/godot/data/sealed_lighthouse.json",
            "src/nesy_game/npc_goal.py",
            "scripts/codex_oauth_llm.py",
            "scripts/run_npc_goal_experiment.py",
            "game-track/godot/scripts/game3d/game_3d.gd",
            "game-track/godot/scripts/game3d/npc_agent_client.gd",
            "game-track/godot/scripts/sealed_lighthouse_machine.gd",
            "game-track/godot/scripts/canonical_state.gd",
            "game-track/godot/scenes/main_3d.tscn",
        )
        for relative in required:
            destination = root / relative
            destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(P / relative, destination)
        return holder, root, packet

    @staticmethod
    def _read_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
        return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if line]

    @staticmethod
    def _write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
        path.write_text(
            "".join(
                json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n" for row in rows
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )

    def _synthetic_packet(
        self, packet_id: str = "NPC-ENG1"
    ) -> tuple[tempfile.TemporaryDirectory[str], Path]:
        holder = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=P)
        packet_dir = Path(holder.name) / "packet"
        packet_dir.mkdir()
        is_engineering = packet_id == "NPC-ENG1"
        packet_name = "engineering" if is_engineering else "screening"
        expected_count = 16 if is_engineering else 72
        cases = load_cases(
            P / f"data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/{packet_name}.json",
            packet_id,
            16 if is_engineering else 12,
        )
        config_digest = canonical_config_hash(P / "configs/npc-goal-recovery.json")
        project_digest = source_project_config_hash(P)
        schema_digest = sha256_file(P / "game-track/schemas/npc-goal-recovery.schema.json")
        frozen_policy = policy_hash(P)
        budget = protocol_budget(
            json.loads((P / "configs/npc-goal-recovery.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        )
        assignments = make_assignments(
            cases,
            packet_id=packet_id,
            config_hash=config_digest,
            npc_protocol_config_sha256=config_digest,
            source_schema_sha256=schema_digest,
            source_project_config_sha256=project_digest,
            policy_hash=frozen_policy,
            budget=budget,
        )
        records = [
            execute_assignment(
                case,
                assignment,
                budget=DEFAULT_BUDGET,
                model=None,
                service_url=None,
                godot_binary=None,
                godot_project=P / "game-track" / "godot",
                output_dir=packet_dir,
                godot_timeout=1,
                skip_model=False,
                admission_failure={
                    "category": "engine_gate_failure",
                    "code": "synthetic_admission",
                    "error": "synthetic",
                },
            )
            for case, assignment in zip(cases, assignments)
        ]
        manifest = {
            "schema_version": "npc-goal-recovery/evidence-1",
            "packet_id": packet_id,
            "config_hash": config_digest,
            "source_project_config_sha256": project_digest,
            "source_schema_sha256": schema_digest,
            "npc_protocol_config_sha256": config_digest,
            "policy_hash": frozen_policy,
            "budget": budget,
            "claim_boundary": dict(EXPECTED_CLAIMS),
            "assignment_count": len(assignments),
            "assignments": assignments,
        }
        manifest_name = (
            "engineering-assignment-manifest.json"
            if is_engineering
            else "screening-assignment-manifest.json"
        )
        result_name = "engineering-results.jsonl" if is_engineering else "screening-results.jsonl"
        aggregate_name = "engineering-aggregate.json" if is_engineering else "aggregate.json"
        (packet_dir / manifest_name).write_text(json.dumps(manifest), encoding="utf-8")
        write_jsonl_preserving(packet_dir / result_name, records)
        packet_aggregate = aggregate(
            records,
            assignments,
            packet_id=packet_id,
            config_hash=config_digest,
            npc_protocol_config_sha256=config_digest,
            source_schema_sha256=schema_digest,
            source_project_config_sha256=project_digest,
            policy_hash=frozen_policy,
        )
        packet_aggregate["run_mode"] = "preflight"
        packet_aggregate["cli_exec_attempt_count"] = 0
        (packet_dir / aggregate_name).write_text(json.dumps(packet_aggregate), encoding="utf-8")
        source_manifest = {
            "schema_version": "npc-goal-recovery/evidence-1",
            "config_hash": config_digest,
            "source_project_config_sha256": project_digest,
            "source_project_config_sha256_after": project_digest,
            "source_project_unchanged": True,
            "npc_protocol_config_sha256": config_digest,
            "source_schema_sha256": schema_digest,
            "policy_hash": frozen_policy,
            "packets": [packet_id],
            "counts": {packet_id: len(assignments)},
            "run_mode": "preflight",
            "cli_exec_attempt_count": 0,
            "artifact_root": str(packet_dir),
            "runtime_source_hashes_before": runtime_source_hashes(P),
            "runtime_source_hashes_after": runtime_source_hashes(P),
            "runtime_sources_unchanged": True,
        }
        (packet_dir / "source-manifest.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(source_manifest), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        (packet_dir / "replay-results.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(replay_report(records, packet_id)), encoding="utf-8"
        )
        write_sha256_manifest(packet_dir)
        self._synthetic_result_name = result_name
        self._synthetic_aggregate_name = aggregate_name
        self._synthetic_packet_id = packet_id
        self.assertEqual(len(assignments), expected_count)
        return holder, packet_dir

    def _validate_synthetic_mutation(
        self, mutate, packet_id: str = "NPC-ENG1"
    ) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
        holder, packet_dir = self._synthetic_packet(packet_id)
        with holder:
            mutate(packet_dir)
            return validate_packet(packet_id, P, packet_dir)

    def test_replay_report_rejects_arbitrary_counts_omitted_duplicates_and_lane_swaps(self) -> None:
        def mutate(packet_dir: Path) -> None:
            replay_path = packet_dir / "replay-results.json"
            payload = json.loads(replay_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            rows = payload["rows"]
            if self._replay_mutation == "arbitrary-count":
                payload["rows"] = rows[:-1]
            elif self._replay_mutation == "omitted-duplicate":
                payload["rows"] = rows[:-1] + [deepcopy(rows[0])]
            else:
                swapped = deepcopy(rows)
                swapped[0]["assignment_id"], swapped[1]["assignment_id"] = (
                    swapped[1]["assignment_id"],
                    swapped[0]["assignment_id"],
                )
                swapped[0]["status"] = "completed"
                swapped[0]["assigned_failure"] = False
                payload["rows"] = swapped
            replay_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
            write_sha256_manifest(packet_dir)

    def test_receipt_rejects_empty_missing_duplicate_and_relocated_entries(self) -> None:
        def mutate(packet_dir: Path) -> None:
            receipt_path = packet_dir / "sha256-manifest.json"
            receipt = json.loads(receipt_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            entries = receipt["files"]
            if self._receipt_mutation == "empty":
                receipt["files"] = []
            elif self._receipt_mutation == "missing":
                receipt["files"] = entries[:-1]
            elif self._receipt_mutation == "duplicate":
                receipt["files"] = entries + [deepcopy(entries[0])]
            else:
                receipt["files"] = entries + [
                    {"path": "../relocated-packet.json", "sha256": "a" * 64}
                ]
            receipt_path.write_text(json.dumps(receipt), encoding="utf-8")

        for variant in ("empty", "missing", "duplicate", "relocated"):
            with self.subTest(variant=variant):
                self._receipt_mutation = variant
                report, errors = self._validate_synthetic_mutation(mutate)
                self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
                self.assertTrue(any("receipt" in error or "sha256" in error for error in errors))

    def test_relocated_packet_file_corruption_is_not_hidden_by_receipt(self) -> None:
        def mutate(packet_dir: Path) -> None:
            source = packet_dir / "engineering-results.jsonl"
            relocated = packet_dir / "relocated" / source.name
            relocated.parent.mkdir()
            source.replace(relocated)

        report, errors = self._validate_synthetic_mutation(mutate)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(any("receipt" in error or "missing" in error for error in errors))

    def test_publication_requires_publish_mode(self) -> None:
        def mutate(packet_dir: Path) -> None:
            for name in ("source-manifest.json", "aggregate.json"):
                path = packet_dir / name
                payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                payload["run_mode"] = "dry_run"
                path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
            write_sha256_manifest(packet_dir)

        report, errors = self._validate_synthetic_mutation(mutate, "NPC-SCR1")
        self.assertEqual(report["run_mode"], "dry_run")
        self.assertFalse(report["publishable"])
        self.assertFalse(any("run_mode" in error for error in errors))

    def test_publication_requires_injected_execution_attempt(self) -> None:
        def mutate(packet_dir: Path) -> None:
            for name in ("source-manifest.json", "aggregate.json"):
                path = packet_dir / name
                payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                payload["run_mode"] = "publish"
                payload["cli_exec_attempt_count"] = 0
                path.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
            write_sha256_manifest(packet_dir)

        report, errors = self._validate_synthetic_mutation(mutate, "NPC-SCR1")
        self.assertEqual(report["run_mode"], "publish")
        self.assertFalse(report["publishable"])
        self.assertTrue(any("exec" in error.lower() for error in errors))

    def test_canonical_final_manifests_have_exact_arm_denominators(self) -> None:
        root = self._verified_dir()
        expected = {
            "engineering-assignment-manifest.json": ("NPC-ENG1", Counter({"R": 16})),
            "screening-assignment-manifest.json": (
                "NPC-SCR1",
                Counter({"P": 24, "R": 24, "O": 24}),
            ),
        }
        for filename, (packet_id, arms) in expected.items():
            with self.subTest(packet=packet_id):
                manifest = json.loads((root / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                assignments = manifest["assignments"]
                self.assertEqual(manifest["packet_id"], packet_id)
                self.assertEqual(len(assignments), sum(arms.values()))
                self.assertEqual(Counter(row["arm"] for row in assignments), arms)
                self.assertTrue(
                    all(
                        str(row["fixture_ref"]).startswith(
                            "data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/"
                        )
                        for row in assignments
                    )
                )

    def test_canonical_packet_survives_relocation_and_rejects_input_substitution(self) -> None:
        canonical = self._verified_dir()
        for packet_id in ("NPC-ENG1", "NPC-SCR1"):
            with self.subTest(location="canonical", packet=packet_id):
                report, errors = validate_packet(packet_id, P, canonical)
                self.assertTrue(report["valid"], errors)

        negative_cases = ("actual_input", "outside_packet", "same_basename", "input_tamper")
        for variant in negative_cases:
            holder, root, packet = self._copy_portable_project()
            with holder, self.subTest(location="relocated", variant=variant):
                if variant == "input_tamper":
                    rows = self._read_jsonl(packet / "engineering-results.jsonl")
                    record = next(
                        row
                        for row in rows
                        if row.get("status") == "completed"
                        and row.get("engine", {}).get("experiment")
                    )
                    input_path = packet / record["engine"]["experiment"]["input_path"]
                    receipt_before = (packet / "sha256-manifest.json").read_bytes()
                    input_path.write_bytes(input_path.read_bytes() + b"\n")
                    report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-ENG1", root, packet)
                    self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
                    self.assertEqual(receipt_before, (packet / "sha256-manifest.json").read_bytes())
                    self.assertTrue(
                        any(
                            "native input SHA does not match actual immutable file" in error
                            for error in errors
                        ),
                        errors,
                    )
                    continue

                rows = self._read_jsonl(packet / "engineering-results.jsonl")
                record = next(
                    row
                    for row in rows
                    if row.get("status") == "completed" and row.get("engine", {}).get("experiment")
                )
                experiment = record["engine"]["experiment"]
                input_name = Path(experiment["input_path"]).name
                command = experiment["command"]
                command_index = command.index("--npc-experiment") + 1
                if variant == "actual_input":
                    replacement = next(
                        path
                        for path in (packet / "godot-input").glob("*.experiment.json")
                        if path.name != input_name
                        and ".tamper." not in path.name
                        and "engine_fixture_reexecution" not in path.name
                    )
                elif variant == "outside_packet":
                    replacement = root / "outside-input.json"
                    replacement.write_text("{}\n", encoding="utf-8")
                else:
                    replacement = packet / "alternate" / input_name
                    replacement.parent.mkdir()
                    shutil.copy2(packet / experiment["input_path"], replacement)
                command[command_index] = str(replacement)
                record["record_sha256"] = digest(
                    {key: value for key, value in record.items() if key != "record_sha256"}
                )
                self._write_jsonl(packet / "engineering-results.jsonl", rows)
                write_sha256_manifest(packet)
                report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-ENG1", root, packet)
                self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
                self.assertTrue(
                    any(
                        "command input does not match metadata.input_path" in error
                        for error in errors
                    ),
                    errors,
                )

    def test_dry_run_or_skip_directory_cannot_publish(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as empty:
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-ENG1", P, Path(empty))
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(any(error.startswith("missing manifest:") for error in errors))

    def test_missing_p_execution_rows_reject_publication(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "screening-results.jsonl"
            rows = self._read_jsonl(path)
            self.assertEqual(sum(row.get("arm") == "P" for row in rows), 24)
            self._write_jsonl(path, [row for row in rows if row.get("arm") != "P"])
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(any("missing assignment results" in error for error in errors))

    def test_record_cannot_rebind_p_assignment_to_rule_arm(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "screening-results.jsonl"
            rows = self._read_jsonl(path)
            record = next(row for row in rows if row.get("arm") == "P")
            record["arm"] = "R"
            record["record_sha256"] = digest(
                {key: value for key, value in record.items() if key != "record_sha256"}
            )
            self._write_jsonl(path, rows)
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(any("assignment identity mismatch for arm" in error for error in errors))

    def test_rehashed_aggregate_counters_cannot_fake_valid_counts(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "aggregate.json"
            aggregate = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            aggregate["arms"]["R"]["n"] += 1
            path.write_text(json.dumps(aggregate, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(
            any("aggregate arm R counts/statuses mismatch" in error for error in errors)
        )

    def test_embedded_native_trace_must_match_packet_scoped_sidecar(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "screening-results.jsonl"
            rows = self._read_jsonl(path)
            record = next(
                row
                for row in rows
                if row.get("status") == "completed"
                and row.get("engine", {}).get("experiment", {}).get("trace")
            )
            record["engine"]["experiment"]["trace"]["passed"] = False
            record["record_sha256"] = digest(
                {key: value for key, value in record.items() if key != "record_sha256"}
            )
            self._write_jsonl(path, rows)
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(
            any("experiment sidecar stage/hash gate failed" in error for error in errors)
        )

    def test_native_input_path_must_stay_inside_packet_and_bind_record(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "screening-results.jsonl"
            rows = self._read_jsonl(path)
            record = next(
                row
                for row in rows
                if row.get("status") == "completed" and row.get("engine", {}).get("experiment")
            )
            record["engine"]["experiment"]["input_path"] = "../../outside.json"
            record["record_sha256"] = digest(
                {key: value for key, value in record.items() if key != "record_sha256"}
            )
            self._write_jsonl(path, rows)
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(
            any(
                "experiment: native input artifact missing or outside packet" in error
                for error in errors
            )
        )

    def test_completed_record_cannot_rebind_request_id(self) -> None:
        holder, packet_dir = self._copy_verified()
        with holder:
            path = packet_dir / "screening-results.jsonl"
            rows = self._read_jsonl(path)
            record = next(
                row
                for row in rows
                if row.get("status") == "completed" and isinstance(row.get("request"), dict)
            )
            record["request"]["request_id"] = "request-mismatch"
            record["record_sha256"] = digest(
                {key: value for key, value in record.items() if key != "record_sha256"}
            )
            self._write_jsonl(path, rows)
            write_sha256_manifest(packet_dir)
            report, errors = validate_packet("NPC-SCR1", P, packet_dir)
        self.assertFalse(report["valid"])
        self.assertTrue(
            any(
                "request" in error.lower()
                and ("identity" in error.lower() or "bind" in error.lower())
                for error in errors
            )
        )

from __future__ import annotations

SHA256 = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
V5 = P / "data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5"


class NpcValidatedV5EvidenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def _packet(self, name: str) -> dict:
        return json.loads((V5 / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_v5_packets_have_frozen_counts_and_portable_case_refs(self) -> None:
        expected = {"engineering.json": 16, "screening.json": 12}
        for filename, count in expected.items():
            with self.subTest(packet=filename):
                packet = self._packet(filename)
                self.assertEqual(len(packet["cases"]), count)
                for case in packet["cases"]:
                    ref = case["fixture_ref"]
                    self.assertTrue(ref.startswith("data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/"))
                    self.assertFalse(Path(ref).is_absolute())
                    self.assertNotIn("..", Path(ref).parts)
                    self.assertTrue(SHA256.fullmatch(case["raw_utterance_sha256"]))
                    self.assertTrue(SHA256.fullmatch(case["fixture_ref_sha256"]))
                    self.assertTrue(SHA256.fullmatch(case["bootstrap_receipt_sha256"]))

    def test_true_goal_case_does_not_fabricate_expected_goal_field(self) -> None:
        packet = self._packet("screening.json")
        case = next(case for case in packet["cases"] if case["id"] == "SCR-D02")
        self.assertTrue(case["observation"]["goal_reached"])
        self.assertNotIn("expected_goal_reached", case)
        self.assertIsNone(case["expected_operation"])
        # The v5 packet is intentionally checked before the validator's v5 path cutover;
        # no derived expected-goal field may be introduced during normalization.

    def test_correction_metadata_proves_pre_model_actual_bootstrap_skip_gate(self) -> None:
        metadata = json.loads((V5 / "correction-manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertTrue(metadata["pre_model"])
        self.assertEqual(metadata["model_calls"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(metadata["integrity"]["receipt_status_required"], "passed")
        self.assertEqual(
            metadata["integrity"]["bootstrap_receipts"],
            "actual_disposable_godot_headless_bootstrap",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            metadata["counts"],
            {"engineering_cases": 16, "screening_cases": 12, "source_independent_fraction": 0},
        )
        self.assertFalse(metadata["claims"]["efficacy"])
        self.assertFalse(metadata["claims"]["human_authored_labels"])

    def test_each_v5_case_binds_actual_bootstrap_receipt_and_source_hashes(self) -> None:
        for filename in ("engineering.json", "screening.json"):
            packet = self._packet(filename)
            for case in packet["cases"]:
                with self.subTest(case=case["id"]):
                    self.assertTrue(case["bootstrap_receipt_ref"].startswith("bootstrap/"))
                    self.assertEqual(case["bootstrap_receipt_ref"].count("/"), 1)
                    self.assertIsInstance(case["source_hashes"], dict)
                    self.assertTrue(
                        all(SHA256.fullmatch(value) for value in case["source_hashes"].values())
                    )
                    self.assertEqual(case["source_kind"], "same_model_family_auxiliary")

    def test_load_cases_admits_only_validated_v5_packets(self) -> None:
        cases = load_cases(V5 / "engineering.json", "NPC-ENG1", 16)
        self.assertEqual(len(cases), 16)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            legacy = Path(directory) / "legacy.json"
            payload = self._packet("engineering.json")
            payload["schema_version"] = "npc-goal-recovery/validated-v4"
            legacy.write_text(json.dumps(payload), encoding="utf-8")
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "validated-v5"):
                load_cases(legacy, "NPC-ENG1", 16)

    def test_cli_dry_run_freezes_v5_manifest_without_staging_or_model_calls(self) -> None:
        script = P / "scripts/run_npc_goal_experiment.py"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            output = Path(directory) / "dry-run"
            completed = subprocess.run(
                [
                    sys.executable,
                    str(script),
                    "--packet",
                    "screening",
                    "--dry-run",
                    "--output-dir",
                    str(output),
                ],
                cwd=P,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                check=False,
                timeout=120,
            )
            self.assertEqual(completed.returncode, 0, completed.stderr)
            manifest = json.loads(
                (output / "screening-assignment-manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )
            self.assertEqual(manifest["schema_version"], "npc-goal-recovery/evidence-1")
            self.assertEqual(manifest["assignment_count"], 72)
            self.assertEqual(manifest["packet_id"], "NPC-SCR1")
            assignments = manifest["assignments"]
            self.assertEqual(len({row["assignment_key"] for row in assignments}), 72)
            self.assertTrue(all(SHA256.fullmatch(row["assignment_key"]) for row in assignments))
            self.assertTrue(
                all(
                    row["fixture_ref"].startswith("data/fixtures/npc-goal-recovery/validated-v5/")
                    for row in assignments
                )
            )
            self.assertTrue(
                all(
                    row["source_kind"] == "same_model_family_auxiliary"
                    and not row["independent_source"]
                    for row in assignments
                )
            )
            self.assertFalse((output / "screening-results.jsonl").exists())
            self.assertFalse((output / "godot-project").exists())
            self.assertEqual(
                json.loads((output / "source-manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[
                    "cli_exec_attempt_count"
                ],
                0,
            )

    def test_cli_preflight_and_dry_run_reject_canonical_publish_directory(self) -> None:
        script = P / "scripts/run_npc_goal_experiment.py"
        canonical = P / "research/academic-pipeline/npc-goal-recovery/verified-run-004"
        for flag in ("--dry-run", "--skip-model"):
            with self.subTest(flag=flag):
                completed = subprocess.run(
                    [
                        sys.executable,
                        str(script),
                        "--packet",
                        "screening",
                        flag,
                        "--output-dir",
                        str(canonical),
                    ],
                    cwd=P,
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    check=False,
                    timeout=120,
                )
                self.assertEqual(completed.returncode, 2)
                self.assertIn("canonical publish output is forbidden", completed.stderr)

    def test_godot_timeout_and_launch_failures_are_assigned_failure_signals(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            input_path = root / "input.json"
            input_path.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
            log_dir = root / "logs"
            sleeper = root / "sleep-godot"
            sleeper.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nsleep 2\n", encoding="utf-8")
            sleeper.chmod(0o755)
            timed_out = run_godot(
                binary=str(sleeper),
                project=P,
                mode="bootstrap",
                input_path=input_path,
                log_dir=log_dir,
                timeout=0.01,
            )
            self.assertEqual(timed_out["status"], "deadline_timeout")
            self.assertEqual(timed_out["error"], "godot_deadline")
            broken = root / "broken-godot"
            broken.write_text("#!/no/such/interpreter\n", encoding="utf-8")
            broken.chmod(0o755)
            launch_failed = run_godot(
                binary=str(broken),
                project=P,
                mode="bootstrap",
                input_path=input_path,
                log_dir=log_dir,
                timeout=1,
            )
            self.assertEqual(launch_failed["status"], "launch_failed")
            self.assertEqual(launch_failed["error"], "FileNotFoundError")

    def test_existing_assignment_result_is_never_overwritten(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            path = Path(directory) / "results.jsonl"
            write_jsonl_preserving(path, [{"assignment_id": "A", "status": "assigned_failure"}])
            write_jsonl_preserving(
                path,
                [
                    {"assignment_id": "A", "status": "completed"},
                    {"assignment_id": "B", "status": "assigned_failure"},
                ],
            )
            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
            self.assertEqual(
                rows,
                [
                    {"assignment_id": "A", "status": "assigned_failure"},
                    {"assignment_id": "B", "status": "assigned_failure"},
                ],
            )

    def test_null_policy_and_snapshot_admission_never_invokes_model(self) -> None:
        cases = load_cases(V5 / "engineering.json", "NPC-ENG1", 16)
        case = cases[0]
        assignment = make_assignments(
            [case],
            packet_id="NPC-ENG1",
            config_hash="a" * 64,
            npc_protocol_config_sha256="b" * 64,
            source_schema_sha256="c" * 64,
            source_project_config_sha256="d" * 64,
            policy_hash=None,
            budget=DEFAULT_BUDGET,
        )[0]
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            record = execute_assignment(
                case,
                assignment,
                budget=DEFAULT_BUDGET,
                model=None,
                service_url=None,
                godot_binary="/definitely/missing-godot",
                godot_project=P / "game-track" / "godot",
                output_dir=Path(directory),
                godot_timeout=1,
                skip_model=False,
            )
        codes = {failure["code"] for failure in record["failure"]}
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertEqual(record["model_calls"], 0)
        self.assertIn("policy_hash_unavailable", codes)
        self.assertIn("public_snapshot_unavailable", codes)

    def _s01_assignment(self) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
        normalized = next(
            case
            for case in load_cases(V5 / "engineering.json", "NPC-ENG1", 16)
            if case["id"] == "ENG-S01"
        )
        assignment = make_assignments(
            [normalized],
            packet_id="NPC-ENG1",
            config_hash=normalized["config_hash"],
            npc_protocol_config_sha256=normalized["config_hash"],
            source_schema_sha256="c" * 64,
            source_project_config_sha256="d" * 64,
            policy_hash=self._packet("engineering.json")["policy_hash"],
            budget=DEFAULT_BUDGET,
        )[0]
        return fixture_case(assignment), assignment

    def _tamper_trace(self, payload: dict, variant: str) -> dict | None:
        initial = payload["initial_public_snapshot"]
        step = dict(payload["decision"]["body"]["steps"][0])
        step["operation"] = "tampered_operation"
        state_ref = deepcopy(initial["state_ref"])
        row = {
            "action_id": step["action_id"],
            "arguments": deepcopy(step["arguments"]),
            "codes": ["CONTENT_HASH_MISMATCH", "STEP_ARGUMENTS_INVALID", "STEP_BINDING_INVALID"],
            "execution_mode": step["execution_mode"],
            "index": 0,
            "observation_after": deepcopy(initial["observation"]),
            "observation_before": deepcopy(initial["observation"]),
            "operation": step["operation"],
            "result": {
                "accepted": False,
                "codes": [
                    "CONTENT_HASH_MISMATCH",
                    "STEP_ARGUMENTS_INVALID",
                    "STEP_BINDING_INVALID",
                ],
                "mutated": False,
            },
            "state_ref_after": deepcopy(state_ref),
            "state_ref_before": deepcopy(state_ref),
            "status": "REJECTED",
            "step": deepcopy(step),
        }
        trace = {
            "actions": [row],
            "errors": ["ACTION_NOT_APPLIED"],
            "final_state_ref": deepcopy(state_ref),
            "initial_public_snapshot": deepcopy(initial),
            "initial_state_ref": deepcopy(state_ref),
            "passed": False,
            "status": "REJECTED",
            "terminal_status": "REJECTED",
        }
        if variant == "missing-sidecar":
            return None
        if variant == "no-rows":
            trace["actions"] = []
        elif variant == "no-rejection":
            trace["actions"] = []
            trace["errors"] = []
            trace["status"] = "FAILED"
            trace["terminal_status"] = "FAILED"
        elif variant == "unexpected-runtime-error":
            trace["errors"] = ["SCRIPT_ERROR"]
            trace["status"] = "FAILED"
            trace["terminal_status"] = "FAILED"
        elif variant == "weak-rejection":
            trace["actions"][0]["codes"] = []
            trace["actions"][0]["result"]["codes"] = []
        elif variant == "applied-row":
            applied = deepcopy(row)
            applied["status"] = "APPLIED"
            applied["result"] = {"accepted": True, "codes": [], "mutated": True}
            applied["codes"] = []
            trace["actions"].append(applied)
        elif variant == "changed-state":
            changed = {"revision": state_ref["revision"] + 1, "sha256": "e" * 64}
            trace["actions"][0]["state_ref_after"] = changed
            trace["final_state_ref"] = deepcopy(changed)
        return trace

    def _execute_s01_tamper(
        self, variant: str, *, returncode: int = 1, ack_variant: str | None = None
    ) -> dict:
        case, assignment = self._s01_assignment()

        def fake_run_godot(*, mode: str, input_path: Path, **_: object) -> dict:
            payload = json.loads(input_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            input_flags = {
                "input_exists": True,
                "input_nonempty": True,
                "input_bytes_preserved": True,
            }
            if mode == "bootstrap":
                initial = {
                    "observation": deepcopy(case["observation"]),
                    "state_ref": deepcopy(case["state_ref"]),
                }
                trace = {
                    "actual_state_ref": deepcopy(case["state_ref"]),
                    "initial_public_snapshot": initial,
                    "initial_state_ref": deepcopy(case["state_ref"]),
                    "observation": deepcopy(case["observation"]),
                    "passed": True,
                    "policy_hash": assignment["policy_hash"],
                    "source_hashes": {"policy_hash": assignment["policy_hash"]},
                }
                return {"status": "completed", "returncode": 0, "trace": trace, **input_flags}
            if ".tamper." in input_path.name:
                trace = self._tamper_trace(payload, variant)
                return {
                    "status": "failed" if returncode else "completed",
                    "returncode": returncode,
                    "trace": trace,
                    **input_flags,
                }
            initial = payload["initial_public_snapshot"]
            step = dict(payload["decision"]["body"]["steps"][0])
            after_observation = deepcopy(initial["observation"])
            after_observation["lens_carried"] = True
            after_observation["lens_reachable"] = False
            after_state = {
                "revision": 1,
                "sha256": "19b474dcc12a962d8a43b0a33ceaa1d845f047dfa8c772e08095dcbfa463c498",
            }
            action = {
                "action_id": step["action_id"],
                "arguments": deepcopy(step["arguments"]),
                "codes": [],
                "execution_mode": step["execution_mode"],
                "index": 0,
                "observation_after": after_observation,
                "observation_before": deepcopy(initial["observation"]),
                "operation": step["operation"],
                "result": {"accepted": True, "codes": [], "mutated": True},
                "state_ref_after": after_state,
                "state_ref_before": deepcopy(initial["state_ref"]),
                "status": "APPLIED",
                "step": deepcopy(step),
            }
            trace = {
                "actions": [action],
                "decision": deepcopy(payload["decision"]),
                "decision_sha256": payload["decision"].get("content_sha256"),
                "errors": [],
                "expected_negative_gates": [],
                "final_state_ref": deepcopy(after_state),
                "initial_public_snapshot": deepcopy(initial),
                "initial_state_ref": deepcopy(initial["state_ref"]),
                "passed": True,
                "status": "APPLIED",
            }
            return {"status": "completed", "returncode": 0, "trace": trace, **input_flags}

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory, ExitStack() as stack:
            stack.enter_context(
                patch.object(experiment_runner, "run_godot", side_effect=fake_run_godot)
            )
            if ack_variant is not None:
                original_handle_result = experiment_runner.GoalController.handle_result

                def fake_handle_result(controller: object, envelope: dict) -> dict | None:
                    if ack_variant == "missing":
                        return None
                    ack = original_handle_result(controller, envelope)
                    if ack_variant == "nonaccepted":
                        mutated = deepcopy(ack)
                        mutated["body"] = {
                            **mutated["body"],
                            "status": "REJECTED",
                            "error": "synthetic_rejection",
                        }
                        mutated["content_sha256"] = experiment_runner.digest(
                            {
                                key: value
                                for key, value in mutated.items()
                                if key != "content_sha256"
                            }
                        )
                        return mutated
                    if ack_variant == "mismatched-request-id":
                        mutated = deepcopy(ack)
                        mutated["request_id"] = "request-mismatch"
                        mutated["content_sha256"] = experiment_runner.digest(
                            {
                                key: value
                                for key, value in mutated.items()
                                if key != "content_sha256"
                            }
                        )
                        return mutated
                    return ack

                stack.enter_context(
                    patch.object(
                        experiment_runner.GoalController,
                        "handle_result",
                        fake_handle_result,
                    )
                )
            return execute_assignment(
                case,
                assignment,
                budget=DEFAULT_BUDGET,
                model=None,
                service_url=None,
                godot_binary="fake-godot",
                godot_project=P / "game-track" / "godot",
                output_dir=Path(directory),
                godot_timeout=1,
                skip_model=False,
            )

    def test_validator_requires_tamper_clone_to_clear_controls_and_preserve_setup(self) -> None:
        state_ref = {"revision": 0, "sha256": "a" * 64}
        observation = {
            "lens_reachable": True,
            "lens_carried": False,
            "signal_restored": False,
            "tide_lead_authorized": False,
            "goal_reached": False,
            "in_dialogue": True,
        }
        snapshot = {"state_ref": state_ref, "observation": observation}
        context = {
            "session_id": "session-test",
            "episode_id": "episode-test",
            "session_epoch": 0,
            "request_id": "request-test",
            "goal_id": experiment_runner.GOAL_ID,
            "goal_version": experiment_runner.GOAL_VERSION,
            "config_hash": "c" * 64,
        }
        decision = {
            "body": {
                "steps": [
                    {
                        "operation": "acquire_object",
                        "action_id": "action-test",
                        "arguments": {},
                        "execution_mode": "npc",
                    }
                ]
            }
        }
        experiment_input = {
            "request_context": context,
            "request": context,
            "decision": decision,
            "initial_public_snapshot": snapshot,
            "setup_operations": [{"operation": "setup"}],
            "seed_world_events": [{"event": "seed"}],
            "mutations": [
                {"code": "no_route"},
                {"code": "fixture-playeraction", "operation": "acquire_object"},
            ],
        }
        expected_hashes = {
            "config_hash": "c" * 64,
            "source_project_config_sha256": "s" * 64,
            "npc_protocol_config_sha256": "c" * 64,
            "source_schema_sha256": "h" * 64,
            "policy_hash": "p" * 64,
        }
        action = {
            "action_id": "action-test",
            "operation": "acquire_object",
            "status": "APPLIED",
            "result": {"accepted": True, "mutated": True},
            "state_ref_before": state_ref,
            "state_ref_after": state_ref,
            "observation_before": observation,
            "observation_after": observation,
        }
        experiment_report = {
            "passed": True,
            "assignment_id": "A",
            "fixture_ref": "fixture#CASE",
            "policy_hash": expected_hashes["policy_hash"],
            "source_hashes": expected_hashes,
            "initial_public_snapshot": snapshot,
            "final_state_ref": state_ref,
            "decision": decision,
            "decision_sha256": evidence_validator.digest(decision),
            "request_context": context,
            "request": context,
            "actions": [action],
            "expected_negative_gates": [],
        }
        tamper_report = {
            "initial_state_ref": state_ref,
            "final_state_ref": state_ref,
            "actions": [
                {**action, "status": "REJECTED", "result": {"accepted": False, "mutated": False}}
            ],
        }

        replay_input = deepcopy(experiment_input)

        def run_variant(variant: str) -> list[str]:
            temporary_paths: list[Path] = []

            tamper_input = deepcopy(experiment_input)
            tamper_input["mutations"] = []
            tamper_input["decision"]["body"]["steps"][0]["operation"] = "tampered_operation"
            if variant == "inherited-controls":
                tamper_input["mutations"] = deepcopy(experiment_input["mutations"])
            elif variant == "changed-setup":
                tamper_input["setup_operations"] = [{"operation": "different-setup"}]
            metadata = {
                "bootstrap": {
                    "status": "completed",
                    "input_bytes_preserved": True,
                    "trace": {
                        "passed": True,
                        "policy_hash": expected_hashes["policy_hash"],
                        "source_hashes": expected_hashes,
                        "state_ref": state_ref,
                    },
                },
                "experiment": {
                    "status": "completed",
                    "input_bytes_preserved": True,
                    "trace": experiment_report,
                },
                "replay": {
                    "replay_kind": "engine_fixture_reexecution",
                    "mode": "experiment",
                    "status": "completed",
                    "trace": experiment_report,
                },
                "tamper_reexecution": {"status": "failed", "returncode": 1, "trace": tamper_report},
                "replay_comparison": {"passed": True},
            }
            payloads = {
                "bootstrap": ({}, metadata["bootstrap"]["trace"]),
                "experiment": (experiment_input, experiment_report),
                "replay": (replay_input, experiment_report),
                "tamper": (tamper_input, tamper_report),
            }

            def stage(_root, _packet, _metadata, stage_name, _where, _errors, _artifact_root):
                payload, sidecar = payloads[stage_name]
                if stage_name in {"experiment", "replay"}:
                    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
                        mode="w", suffix=".json", delete=False
                    ) as handle:
                        handle.write(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True))
                        path = Path(handle.name)
                    temporary_paths.append(path)
                    return path, None, sidecar, payload
                return None, None, sidecar, payload

            record = {
                "status": "completed",
                "assignment_id": "A",
                "state_ref_before": state_ref,
                "state_ref_after": state_ref,
                "observation_before": observation,
                "decision": decision,
                "engine": metadata,
            }
            assignment = {"assignment_id": "A", "fixture_ref": "fixture#CASE"}
            errors: list[str] = []
            with (
                patch.object(
                    evidence_validator,
                    "fixture_case",
                    return_value={
                        "state_ref": state_ref,
                        "observation": observation,
                        "mutations": [],
                    },
                ),
                patch.object(evidence_validator, "config_hash", return_value="c" * 64),
                patch.object(
                    evidence_validator, "source_project_config_hash", return_value="s" * 64
                ),
                patch.object(evidence_validator, "sha256_file", return_value="h" * 64),
                patch.object(evidence_validator, "_stage_files", side_effect=stage),
                patch.object(evidence_validator, "_validate_native_input"),
                patch.object(evidence_validator, "_validate_action_transition"),
                patch.object(
                    evidence_validator,
                    "_engine_reexecution_comparison",
                    return_value={"passed": True},
                ),
                patch.object(
                    evidence_validator, "_tamper_rejected_from_evidence", return_value=True
                ),
            ):
                evidence_validator._validate_engine(
                    record, assignment, errors, "record[0]", expected_hashes["policy_hash"], P, P
                )
            for path in temporary_paths:
                path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            return errors

        valid_errors = run_variant("valid")
        self.assertFalse(
            any(
                "tamper input did not isolate" in error or "tamper probe must omit" in error
                for error in valid_errors
            )
        )
        self.assertFalse(any("native replay input bytes differ" in error for error in valid_errors))
        inherited_errors = run_variant("inherited-controls")
        self.assertTrue(
            any(
                "tamper probe must omit inherited fixture mutations" in error
                for error in inherited_errors
            )
        )
        changed_setup_errors = run_variant("changed-setup")
        self.assertTrue(
            any(
                "tamper input did not isolate one decision operation change" in error
                for error in changed_setup_errors
            )
        )

    def test_core_replay_rebases_result_metadata_without_relaxing_identity(self) -> None:
        source = {
            "message_type": "result",
            "session_id": "session-1",
            "request_id": "request-1",
            "event_id": "event-1",
            "body": {
                "action_id": "action-1",
                "operation": "acquire_object",
                "status": "APPLIED",
            },
            "sequence": 3,
            "delivery_index": 3,
            "content_sha256": "stale-content-hash",
            "previous_hash": "stale-previous-hash",
            "hash": "stale-record-hash",
        }
        replay_row = deepcopy(source)
        replay_row["sequence"] = 2
        replay_row["delivery_index"] = 2
        replay_row["previous_hash"] = "0" * 64

        rebased = _core_rebased_replay_envelope(source, replay_row)
        content = deepcopy(source)
        for key in ("hash", "previous_hash", "content_sha256"):
            content.pop(key, None)
        content["sequence"] = 2
        content["delivery_index"] = 2
        content_sha256 = digest(content)
        self.assertEqual(rebased["content_sha256"], content_sha256)
        content["content_sha256"] = content_sha256
        self.assertEqual(
            rebased["hash"],
            digest(
                {
                    "record": {
                        key: value
                        for key, value in content.items()
                        if key not in {"hash", "previous_hash"}
                    },
                    "previous_hash": "0" * 64,
                }
            ),
        )
        self.assertEqual(rebased["body"], source["body"])
        self.assertEqual(rebased["request_id"], source["request_id"])
        changed_source = deepcopy(source)
        changed_source["body"]["operation"] = "tampered_operation"
        self.assertNotEqual(
            _core_rebased_replay_envelope(changed_source, replay_row),
            rebased,
        )

    def test_exit_one_canonical_rejection_is_successful_negative_gate(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("valid")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "completed")
        self.assertTrue(record["replay"]["godot_tamper_rejected"])
        self.assertNotIn(
            "tamper_replay_evidence_missing", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]}
        )
        self.assertEqual(record["engine"]["tamper_reexecution"]["returncode"], 1)

    def test_exit_one_missing_or_invalid_negative_evidence_remains_assigned_failure(self) -> None:
        for variant in ("missing-sidecar", "no-rows", "no-rejection", "unexpected-runtime-error"):
            with self.subTest(variant=variant):
                record = self._execute_s01_tamper(variant)
                self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
                self.assertFalse(record["replay"].get("godot_tamper_rejected", False))

    def test_missing_first_result_ack_is_assigned_failure(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("valid", ack_variant="missing")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertIn("result_ack_not_accepted", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]})

    def test_nonaccepted_first_result_ack_is_assigned_failure(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("valid", ack_variant="nonaccepted")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertIn("result_ack_not_accepted", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]})

    def test_validator_rejects_missing_or_nonaccepted_first_ack(self) -> None:
        for variant in ("missing", "nonaccepted"):
            with self.subTest(variant=variant):
                record = self._execute_s01_tamper("valid")
                if variant == "missing":
                    record["acks"] = []
                else:
                    record["acks"][0]["body"]["status"] = "REJECTED"
                errors: list[str] = []
                _validate_result_ack_contract(record, "record[0]", errors)
                self.assertTrue(
                    any(
                        "ACK" in error
                        and ("count mismatch" in error or "first result ACK" in error)
                        for error in errors
                    )
                )

    def test_validator_rejects_mismatched_ack_request_id(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("valid")
        self.assertTrue(record["acks"])
        record["acks"][0]["request_id"] = "request-mismatch"
        errors: list[str] = []
        _validate_result_ack_contract(record, "record[0]", errors)
        self.assertTrue(any("ACK[0] identity mismatch for request_id" in error for error in errors))

    def test_crashed_tamper_with_rejected_row_is_not_canonical_rejection(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("unexpected-runtime-error", returncode=1)
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertFalse(record["replay"].get("godot_tamper_rejected", False))

    def test_exit_one_rejection_without_canonical_codes_is_not_evidence(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("weak-rejection")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertFalse(record["replay"]["godot_tamper_rejected"])
        self.assertIn(
            "tamper_replay_evidence_missing", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]}
        )

    def test_any_applied_tamper_row_fails_even_with_rejected_row(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("applied-row")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertFalse(record["replay"]["godot_tamper_rejected"])
        self.assertIn("semantic_tamper_accepted", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]})

    def test_changed_canonical_state_fails_negative_gate(self) -> None:
        record = self._execute_s01_tamper("changed-state")
        self.assertEqual(record["status"], "assigned_failure")
        self.assertFalse(record["replay"]["godot_tamper_rejected"])
        self.assertIn(
            "tamper_replay_evidence_missing", {item["code"] for item in record["failure"]}
        )

    def test_python_and_godot_replay_denominators_remain_independent(self) -> None:
        report = replay_report(
            [
                {
                    "assignment_id": "P",
                    "status": "assigned_failure",
                    "replay": {"python": {"valid": True, "rows": 2}},
                    "engine": {
                        "replay": {"available": True, "status": "completed"},
                        "replay_comparison": {"passed": True},
                    },
                },
                {
                    "assignment_id": "G",
                    "status": "assigned_failure",
                    "replay": {"python": {"valid": False}},
                    "results": [{"x": 1}],
                    "engine": {
                        "replay": {"available": True, "status": "failed"},
                        "replay_comparison": {"passed": False},
                    },
                },
                {
                    "assignment_id": "U",
                    "status": "assigned_failure",
                    "replay": {},
                    "engine": {"replay": {"available": False}},
                },
            ],
            "NPC-ENG1",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            report["denominators"],
            {
                "python_eligible": 1,
                "python_failed": 1,
                "python_unavailable": 1,
                "godot_eligible": 1,
                "godot_failed": 1,
                "godot_unavailable": 1,
            },
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
